eṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum pāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  itippōl āḻvārkaḷum, iṟivaṉ icik karuvikaḷōṭum pāṭuvati kēṭṭu iṉpam nalkupavaṉ eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
tamiḻ iṉamum, tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻiciyum 10 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟivarukiṟārkaḷ.  ātiyicikaḷ cumār 12,000 eṉpar tamiḻ icip pulavarkaḷ.  nāppaṭiyāl urikkap paṭṭa patiṉōrāyirattu toḷḷāyirattu toṇaṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya āti icikaḷ eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāra uriyil kūṟi iruppatik koṇṭu paṇṭiyat tamiḻar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṉ ciṟappāl kaṇṭa 1199-il tamiḻar aracu vīḻntamiyālum tamiḻ icip pulavarkaḷiṉ akkaṟi iṉmiyālum iciyip pēṇātu aḻiyumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.  tēvāra āciriyar kālattiṟku
muṉ 103 - paṇkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  avikaḷilum palamaṟintu viṭṭaṉa.  tēvāra āciriyar tam pāṭalkaḷukku pāṇarkaḷik koṇṭu paṇ amittaṉar.  ataṉāl appar, campantar kālattil 23  - tamiḻp paṇkaḷ uyirōṭu nilaviṉa eṉṟu tiriyamāka kūṟalām.  civaneṟi marutanilattil tōṉṟi taṉakkuriya yāḻiyum muraciyum 23 - tamiḻp paṇkaḷiyum pāṭik kāttu vantatu pōl mulli nilattil tōṉṟiya tirumāl neṟi mulliyāḻiyum mullip paṟiyiyum tiruvāy moḻikkāka vakukkappaṭṭa paṇkaḷiyum pēṇip pātukāttu vantatu.  tiruvāy moḻippāṭalkaḷiṉ nūṟpatippiṉuḷḷē pāṭalukkuriya paṇṇum irākamum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tiruvāy moḻiyil, tēvārattil uḷḷa 23 - paṇkaḷil 13 paṇkaḷ maṭṭum itil uḷḷaṉa.  iḵtiṉṟi tēvārattil kuṟippiṭāta 6 paṇkaḷ tiruvāy moḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ mūlamum tiruvāy moḻi āciriyarkaḷ mūlamum nām 29 paṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kāṇ
kiṟōm.  inta 29 paṇkaḷil 15 tamiḻp paṇkaḷi marapu vaḻuvātu pāṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭil āḷ uṇṭā eṉpatu aiyam.
tirunelvēli nelliyappar- kāntimati ammaṉ kōyilil cāraṅkiyum, pāṇiyum uḷḷatu.  mīṭṭuvārilli.  cāmi caṉṉitiyilum, ammaṉ cannitiyilum icit tūṇkaḷ mīṭṭuvāriṉṟi niṉṟaṉa.  ematu aṉpiṟkum matippiṟkum uriya eṉatu uṟaviṉarum tōḻarumāṉa icimaṇi tiru. kā. caṅkaraṉār atit taṭṭi curam pāṭac ceytu ṭēp rikkāṭum- icip pativum ceytuḷḷār.  tirucci vāṉoli niliyam ati oli parappiyuḷḷatu.  palar atik kēṭṭirukka
māṭṭārkaḷ.  virivil tirunelvēli vāṉoli niliyam mīṇṭum pativu ceytu tirunelvēli, kaṉṉiyākumari, irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭa makkaḷ kēṭṭu iṉpuṟumāṟu virivil ceyyum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṟēṉ.  nelliyappar kōyilil pāṇi eṉṉum tōl karuviyum uḷḷatu.
tiruvaraṅkam kōyilil iṉṟum paḻiya icik karuvikaḷil oṉṟāṉa takki irukkiṟatu.  vācikkappaṭṭum varukiṟatu.  takki tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ kālattil iruntuvarum oru paḻiya tōṟ karuviyākum.  itu iṭi curuṅkiya paṟi iṉattic cērntatu.  takki eṉṉum tōṟkaruviyi iṉṟuḷḷa pala icip pulavarkaḷ kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyārkaḷ.
ikkaruvi ovvoru āṇṭilum tiruvaraṅkattil tirumāl kōyilil kārttikit tiṅkaḷil amāvāci kaḻitta 16 - ām nāḷil kavucik ēkātaciyil ariyar tiru neṭuntāṇṭavam cēvippār.  appoḻutu takki tuṇik karuviyāka vācikkap paṭum.  mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷil varum amāvāci kaḻitta 14  -ām nāḷ vikuṇṭa ēkātaciyil ariyar tivviyap pirapantam  cēvittu āṭum poḻutu takki tuṇikkaruviyāka vācikkappaṭum.  iṅku tavaḷi mattaḷamum tuṇik karuviyāka vācikkappaṭukiṟatu.  ottu muṭintatum patikat toṭakkattiṟku takki vācikkappaṭum.  piṉ aṟuti vittu muṭikkappaṭum.  appāl cutta mattaḷam takki eṉa māṟi māṟi vācippatu marapu.  iṉṟu tiruvaraṅkattil cītārāmaṉ makaṉ kumāracāmi takki vācittu varukiṟār.  vēṟu eṅkum takki iruppatāka eṉakkut teriyavilli.
kuṭa muḻāvum pañcamuka muracum
 tamiḻ nāṭṭil pañca muka muracip pārppatu aritu.  mottattil nāṭṭil nāṉkiṟku mēl kāṇpataritu.  itaṉi aimuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum mattaḷatti irumuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum puṣkaratti mummuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum kuṭamuḻāvi orumuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  pañcamuka muracu tiruvārūrk kōyilum tiruttuṟip pūṇṭi, tiruvāṉikkā kōyilkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.  oru periya kutil pōṉṟa periya vaṭivil 5 nāḷi pōṉṟa mukattil tōl kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum.  itaṉip pañca muracu eṉpar.  itu muṉṉāḷil maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭatu.  ippoḻutu ulōkat takaṭukaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭu icikkappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṟkut tuṇik karuviyāka kuṭamuḻā vācikkappaṭukiṟatu.  itu cuṭu maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu mēlē tōl pōrttappaṭṭirukkum. 
iciyum icik karuvikaḷum
eṉavē tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil vaṭamoḻi mantiraṅkaḷōṭum tēvāram, tiruvācaka, tiruvāy moḻi mutaliya paktip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  civak kōyilkaḷil tēvāram tamiḻ paṇkaḷōṭu pāṭuvataṟku ōtuvārkaḷ paṇṭu toṭṭu pāṭuppaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ōtuvārkaḷukku kōyiliruntu māṉiyaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  palaviṭaṅkaḷil tēvāram pāṭacāli niṟuvi makkaḷukkup paṇṉōṭu pāṭa payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtaṉṟi ellāk kōyilkaḷilum nākacuram, mēḷam, ottu ūtutal tāḷak karuvikaḷ mīṭṭal tavaṟātu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tirunelvēli nelliyappar - kāntimati ammaṉ kōyilil tēvāram ōtavum, cāraṅki eṉṉum icikkaruviyi mīṭṭavum, vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷi ōtavum nelliyil icimaṇi, cā. cuntara ōtuvāriṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kāñciyiliruntu aḻittu varappaṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷum ōtap payiṉṟiruntatāl avari ākama civar eṉṟu avarkaḷukkup paṭṭayam aḷikkap peṟṟiruntatu.  nelli tiru. cuntara ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ tantiyār cālivāṭīcuvara ōtuvāmūrtti avarkaḷ tēvāram maṭṭum ōtum ōtuvārāka iṉṟi iciyum tamiḻp pulamiyum peṟṟavarkaḷāvar.  cumār 100 - āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tirunelvēli muttamiḻc caṅkattil talimip pulavarāyum iruntārkaḷ.  tirucālivāṭīcuvaramūrtti avarkaḷum avarkaḷ makaṉ cuntara ōtuvā mūrtti avarkaḷum tirunelvēli nelliyappar - kāntimati kōyilil parampariyāyt tēvāram pāṭi māṉiyam peṟṟu vanta ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ kuṭumpattic cērntavarkaḷ āvar.  avarkaḷ kuṭumpattic cērntavarkaḷ ampā camuttiram, kaṭiyam mutaliya ūrkaḷil ciṟanta muṟiyil tēvārattip paṇṇōṭu pāṭum ōtuvārkaḷākavum cāraṅki mīṭṭum icippulavarkaḷākavum iruntu vantaṉar.  ippoḻutum āṅkāṅkē avarkaḷ kuṭumpattil palar iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl kōyilil tēvāram ōtum toḻilip palar viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.  āṉālum cuntara ōtuvāmūrttikaḷiṉ pēttimārkaḷ intirāvum, vaḷḷināyakiyum icippayiṉṟu āṅkāṅkē tēvārak kaccēri ceytu varuvatu civa camayapaktarkaḷ, ippeṇ makkaḷ taṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa tēvāra iṉṉici marapip pātukāttu varuvatu kaṇṭumakiḻcci aṭintu vāḻtti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
pañca vāttiyam
kēraḷa nāṭṭil kōyilkaḷil naṭipeṟum uṟcava kālaṅkaḷil pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  pañca vāttiyam tēva vāttiyam eṉpar.  muṉṉāḷil timili, iṭakki, caṅku muraḷi (mukavīṇi) mirutaṅkam ākiyavi pañca vāttiyam eṉappaṭum.  paḻaṅkālattil pañca vāttiyattiṉ aṅkamākap pāṭṭum iruntuvantatu.  aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟi kēraḷa aṟiñarkaḷ māṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  mirutaṅkattiṟkup patilāka cutta mattaḷamum muraḷikkup patilākap pāṇṭi mēḷattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum kompum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.  pañca vāttiyattil tiripuṭatāḷam, nāṉku kālamuḷḷa orupallavi  curampāṭuvatu ākiyavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu pañca vāttiyattiṉ tāḷakkaṭṭu ciṭṭippaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  tiripuṭatāḷattil nāṉku kālamāka pañcavāttiyam icikkap
paṭṭatu.  mutal kālam 56 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, iraṇṭām kālam 23 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, mūṉṟām kālam 14 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, nāṉkām kālam 7 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam eṉṟu muṟip
paṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.  iṟutiyil vāttiyam ēka tāḷattil muṭiyum.
pañca vāttiyatti tiripuṭatāḷattil maṭṭum vācikkappaṭa
vēṇṭumā?  eṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil oru viṉā eḻuntatu.  campi rūpakam mutaliya tāḷattilum vācikkalām.  kaḷippūṭṭum intak kūṭṭu vāttiyatti putu vaḻikaḷil vācittu putuvaḻikaḷik kāṇpatil tavaṟilli pala tamiḻ icip pulavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
ippoḻutu vācikkum aintu vāttiyaṅkaḷil kūṭa cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyyalām eṉac cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ.  pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷil kompum cuttamattaḷamum cērttatāl ācuramāka māṟiviṭṭatu.  cuttamattaḷattiṟkup patilāka mīṇṭum mattaḷam iṭam peṟalām.  cuttamattaḷattiṉ nuṉiyiṉ muḻakkattil timili yiṉ kātukku  iṉiya takāramum tōṅkāramum iṭakkiyiṉ iṉiya cila camaya cevikkup pulappaṭātu pōyviṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉimiyum mirutuvumāṉa teyvat taṉmi vāynta pañca vāttiyattiṟku mirutaṅkam mikavum ēṟpuṭittatu.
kompukkup patilāka nākacurattip payaṉpaṭuttalām.  itil caṅku vēṇṭām.  atu curutikkāka maṭṭum irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇap
paṭukiṟatu.  atu cariyalla.  tiripuṭi, campi, rūpakam ākiyatāḷaṅ
kaḷil caṅki vācippatu nallatu.  tiruppatikkōyil caṅkit tiṟamiyuṭaṉ vācippati palar aṟintu pārāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar aṅku ceṉṟu nāmum atik kaṟṟu varalām.  iṭakki, timili, mirutaṅkam, nātacuram , caṅku ākiyavikaḷ koṇṭa aintu vāttiyaṅkaḷ iṇinta kūṭṭu vāttiyam ciṟantateṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.
icikkaruvikaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭil caṅka kālattil 1000ttiṟkumēṟpaṭṭa tōṟkaruvikaḷum nūṟṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa narampuk karuvikaḷum 50kkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻaṟkaruvikaḷum 15kkum mēṟpaṭṭa karuvikaḷum iruntaṉa.  cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār cumār 31 tōṟkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷic coṉṉār.  aintu nilattiṟkum aintu narampuk karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷic coṉṉār.  eṭṭu kuḻalkaḷiyum, 4 kañcak karuvikaḷiyum cilar eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar.
icik karuvikaḷ tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷil pūcik kālattup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  ivi ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē kōyilkaḷil iruntu vantaṉa.  itaṟku tēvāram tiruvācakam mutaliya nūṟkaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa.  aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟum 31 - tōṟkaruvikaḷil illāta pala tōṟkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṭa tēvāraṅkaḷilum tiruvācakattilum piṟa nāṉku tirumuṟikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. kōyilpūcikku aṣṭāṣa vāttiyaṅkaḷ 18 payaṉ
paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avi muṟiyē 1. pēriki 2. tamarukam 3. tavil 4. tuntiṉā 5. tuntupi 6. kākalam 7. mattaḷam 8. maṭṭukkam 9. nupurā 10. pāṉakam 11. paṭakam 12. captam 13. tāḷam 14. tampuru 15. turiyam 
16. vēṇu 17. vīṇi 18. mirutaṅkam eṉappaṭum.  kōyil muracukaḷ 26.  iliṅkāyata camaya aṟiñarkaḷ vivēka cūṭāmaṇi eṉṉum nūlil pañcamakācaptam, ciriṅkā, vammalatā, caṅkapēri ceyakōcā eṉṟa olikaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉpar.  kōyil muracu 26 iṉ peyarkaḷ:  1. pañcamuka vāttiyam 2. nakarā 3. tamarukam 4. uṭukki 5. tavaṇṭi 6. pampi 7. kaṉakatappaṭṭi 8. uṭal 9. periya uṭal 10. ciṉṉa uṭal 11. vīravaṇṭi 12. cūriyappiṟi, cantirappiṟi 13. timili 
14. ceṇṭi 15. cutta mattaḷam 16. iṭakki 17. cakki, 18. muṭṭu 19. takkā 20. vīramattaḷam  21. takki 22. ceya pēriki 23. ṭaṅkā 24. ṭaṅki 25. vīramallāri 26. tappup palaki eṉpaṉavākum.
tōṟkaruvikaḷ pala uḷa.  ivikaḷi iṟivaṉē icit
tāṉ eṉṟu tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
narampuk karuvikaḷ
paṇṭik kālat tamiḻarkaṇṭa narampuk karuvikaḷil yāḻ, vīṇi, kiṉṉaram, tumpuru, tampurā, kuḻalcaṅku, kañcakkaruvi pōṉṟavikaḷ ciṟappāṉa karuvikaḷākum.  yāḻ vīṇi kuḻal pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ makkaḷi maṭṭumalla vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uḷḷattiyum īrkkavallatu.  yāḻ teyvat taṉmi vāyntatu.  ataṉi aṟiñarkaḷ tīñcuvi nalkum teyva nal yāḻ eṉṟārkaḷ.  civa perumāṉē yāḻi mīṭṭi vantār.  vīṇiyi icittu vantār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  appar aṭikaḷ 
aṟi kalanta mutal monti vīṇi yāḻum
paṇṇoṭu yāḻ vīṇi payiṉṟāyp pōṟṟi
ēḻici yāḻvīṇimuralak kaṇṭēṉ
paṇṇārnta vīṇi payiṉṟavaṉē   		(appar)
eṉṟu tiruppācurattālum iṟivaṉ yāḻmuṟinātaṉ eṉṟum iṟivi yāḻ moḻiyāḷ , yāḻār moḻimaṅki eṉṟu iruttalālum iṟivaṉ vīṇātara taṭcaṇā mūrttiyāyum, tirumāl eppoḻutum tam vāyil vēyṅkuḻali vittu ūtik koṇṭiruppatālum umātēvikku ēḻici vallapi eṉṟa peyar iruttalālum kalimakaḷ tam kiyil vīṇiyit tāṅki iruppatālum piḷḷiyār kūṭa tam kiyil vīṇiyit tāṅki āṭik koṇṭiruppatālum namatu icik karuvikaḷ teyvat taṉmi vāyntavikaḷ eṉṟu aṟivatōṭu aṉṉiyar āṭciyi yālum namatu makkaḷiṉ aṟiviṉmiyālum teyvīka nalyāḻ uḷpaṭa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa icikkaruvikaḷi iṉṟu iḻanta niliyil irukkiṉṟōm.  mīṇṭum namatu āṭciyil avaṟṟi yellām uyirppikka muyalvōmāka.
nāṭṭiyamum tāṇṭavamum
umātēvi āṭiṉāl atu vāciyam.  civaperumāṉ, āṭiṉār tāṇṭavam.  āṇ, vēṭamiṭṭu āṭuvatu nāṭṭiyam.  āṇ peṇ vēṭamiṭṭum peṇ āṇ vēṭamiṭṭu āṭuvatum niruttam muṟkālattē tamiḻakattil āṅkāṅkē kaṉṉi māṭam amittu tirumaṇa vāḻkkiyi virumpāta makaḷirum, iḷavayatil kimiyaṭinta mātarum, peṇṇum illātavāṟu aliyāy tōṉṟiya mātarum kaṉṉimāṭattil cērntu vāḻntuvantār.  aṅkuḷḷa kōyilum civa makkaḷum anta kaṉṉimāṭattiṟku vēṇṭiya utavikaḷ ceytu vantaṉar.  kaṉṉimāṭam civa maṅkiyarkaḷ tavira camaṇa maṅkiyarkaḷiyō cākkiya camayap peṇmakkaḷiyō cērttuk koḷḷātu.  teḷivākac coṉṉāl itu civacamayak kaṉṉiyar maṭam eṉṟu kūṟalām.  ivvāṟu āṭavarkaḷukkum taṉiyē pala ūrkaḷil namatu tiruvāṭutuṟi tiruppaṉantāḻ, tarmapuram pōṉṟu pala maṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
kaṉṉiyar maṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ civatīṭci peṟṟavarkaḷ. atikāliyil eḻuntu nīril mūḻki kōyilukku eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷa nantavaṉattil picci mulli iruvācci campaṅki mutaliya malarkaḷik koytu māliyākat toṭuppārkaḷ.  tirukkōyili meḻukuvārkaḷ.  kōlam pōṭuvārkaḷ.  malar mālikaḷi iṟivaṉukkuc cūṭṭumpaṭi pūcārikaḷiṭam koṭuppārkaḷ.  iṟivaṉmītu paktippāṭalkaḷip pāṭuvārkaḷ.  iṟivaṉ cannitāṉattiṉmuṉ āṭuvārkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ puṉitamāṉa vāḻvu naṭattum civa makaḷir āvar.  ivarkaḷ mītu evarum evvita kuṟṟamum kūṟārkaḷ.  kūṟuvataṟku akkālattu civa naṉmakkaḷō aracō ciṟitum iṭaṅkoṭātu avarkaḷiṉ tūymiyāṉa naṭattiyiyum puṉita civa ulakam pārāṭṭivantatu.
taṉakku maṇam pēci irunta oruvar pōril iṟantupaṭṭār eṉpati aṟinta appar aṭikaḷiṉ akkiyar tilakavatiyār taṉakkut tirumaṇam vēṇṭām eṉṟu maṇavāḻkkiyit tuṟantu kaṉṉimāṭattil cērntu aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum paṭṭāṭi kaḷiyum pakaṭṭāṉa āṭikaḷiyum aṇiyātu eḷiya veṇmi yāṉa āṭikaḷi aṇintuvantār.  avvammiyārkku tāyum illi, tantiyum illi.  tampi oruvar maruḷnīkkiyār maṭṭum uṇṭu.  avar iḷam paruvattilē iḷamiyum yākkiyum niliyātu eṉṟu eṇṇi nallaṟaṅkaḷ pala ceytu varumpoḻutu camaṇa camaya muṉivarkaḷāl pāṭaliputtiram eṉṟa ūril uḷḷa camaṇa maṭattiṟku aḻittu avari camaṇa camayattiṟku cērttu accamaya nūṟkaḷi naṉku payiṟṟuvittu taruma cēṉar eṉṉum ciṟappuppaṭṭamum aḷittaṉar.  tarumacēṉar eṉṟa maruḷnīkkiyār putta camayattiṉaril orucārārākiya cērarkaḷi vātil veṉṟu camaṇa camayat talivarkaḷil mēmpaṭṭavarāy uyarntu viḷaṅkiṉār.
tilakavatiyār tiruvatiki tiru vīraṭṭāṉattirukkōyili aṭintu civa ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aṇintu tampi maṉam māṟi civa camayattiṟku varumāṟu ceyya nāṭōṟum civaperumāṉi vēṇṭit toḻutu vantār.  tirukkōyilil tiruviḷakkiṭutal, cāṇattāl meḻukutal, malar koytu koṇṭu vantu mālikaḷ toṭuttal mutaliya tiruppaṇikaḷi ceytu vantār.  taruma cēṉar cūli nōyāl varunti akkiyiṭam vantu cērntār. tilakavatiyār vatinta kaṉṉiyarmāṭattiṟku vantu tamakkiyar kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkit taṉ nōyi nīkkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉār.  tilakavatiyār civaṉi niṉintu ainteḻuttōti tirunīṟṟik koṭuttaruḷiṉār.  viṭiyaṟkāliyil tiruvatiki vīraṭṭāṉē cuvarar tirukkōyilukku aḻittuc ceṉṟār.  iṟivaṉi vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉār.  tamiḻ mālikaḷākiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷip pāṭum uṇarvu untiyeḻuntatu.  taṉṉip pīṭitta cūli nōy nīṅkiyatu.
ulaki viṭṭu kiṭṭat taṭṭa maṟintu pōṉa civa neṟiyi mutalil toṇṭi nāṭṭilum paravac ceyta tarumacēṉari, tirunāvukkaracar eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ civa camayat toṇṭarākkiya perumi oru vēḷāḷap peṇmaṇiyākiya tilakavatiyāriyē cērum.  atēpōl pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil camaṇa camaya iruḷ niṟintu kiṭanta poḻutu pāṇṭiya aracaṉi campantar mūlam cura nōkki civa oḷi parappiya perumi maṅkiyarkkaraciyāriyē cērum.
tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ piṟanāṭṭup peṇkaḷi viṭa nallavarkaḷ uṟutiyāṉa camayap paṟṟuṭiyavarkaḷ tūymiyāṉa oḻukkamum puṉitamāṉa uḷḷamum peṟṟavarkaḷ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ turatiṣṭam namatu puṉita kaṉṉimaṭatti cila paṇṭāraṅkaḷum, pūcārip pullarkaḷum civa vēṭam tāṅki civa paktarkaḷiṉ icciyit tīrppatum civa tiruppaṇiyil oṉṟu eṉṟu ēmāṟṟi kaṉṉimāṭattil uḷḷa peṇkaḷi vañcittu avarkaḷukkup poṉṉum maṇiyum paṭṭāṭiyum koṭuttu vañcittu kāntiyaṭikaḷ kūṟiyatu pōl vaḻukki viḻunta mātarkaḷākki - atāvatu potu makaḷirākki kaṉṉimāṭaṅkaḷi aḻittu viṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷ tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum paṇiviṭiyāl tēvaṭiyāḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉar.  tēvaṭiyāḷ eṉpatu teyvat toṇṭu ceyyum puṉitap peṇ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.  itaṉ ciṟappi aṟintē māmaṉṉaṉākiya irācarācacōḻaṉ maṉivi mātēvaṭiyāḷ eṉṟum peyarit tāṅki iruntāḷ.  avaḷ pativirati puṉitamāṉa paṭṭattaraci ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷi kōyilukku māṉiyamāka aḷittāḷ.  iṉṟu avaḷatu pēttiyō allatu avaḷ vaḻiyil vanta oru aracakulap peṇ ulakappukaḻ peṟṟa māperum maṉṉaṉ irācarācaṉ maṉiviyiṉ ciṟanta peyari vikka nāṇumpaṭi civap perumakkaḷiṉ tīcceyal tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ peyari keṭuttuviṭṭatu.  civa kaṉṉimāṭattil  uḷḷa peṇkaḷ evvaḷavō keṭṭaḻintu pōṉa kālattum avarkaḷ tamiḻicikkum, nāṭṭiyakkalikkum āṟṟiya toṇṭi tamiḻulakam maṟakka muṭiyātu.  ippeṇ maṇikaḷ nam camayattiṟku peruntoṇṭāṟṟi avarkaḷiṉ icic ciṟappālum nāṭṭiyat tiṟamiyālum iṉṟu kōṭi kōṭiyāy paṇantiraṭṭi ettaṉi makaḷir kallūrikaḷ, nāṭṭiyakkalācāli niṟuviyirukkamuṭiyum.  civa camaya makkaḷ namatu kaṉṉimāṭap peṇkaḷiṉ kaṟpic cūṟiyāṭiyavarkaḷ maṭātipatikaḷāyiṉum, pūcārikaḷāyiṉum, tarumakarttākkaḷāyiṉum avarkaḷ ceyta maṉṉikkamuṭiyāta kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku kaḻuvāyt tēṭiyē tīravēṇṭum. tavaṟu namatu nāyaṉmārkaḷiṭattiliruntālum teyvattiṭam iruntālum inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ viññāṉa ulakam avarkaḷiṉ tīya ceyalik kaṇṭu muṉiyāy irātu maṉṉippum aḷikkātu.
namatu tirukkōyilkaḷi namatu makkaḷum, aracum kavaṉit
tiruntāl namatu ici aḻintirātu 103 paṇkaḷāvatu nilit
tirukkum.  1000 - icik karuvikaḷāvatu kōyilil pātukāttu vikkappaṭṭirukkum.  tāṇṭavamum lākiyamum kūttukaḷum vaḷarcci peṟṟirukkum.
5000 - m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ekiptum, mecapottāmiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum parmā, tāylāntu, kampōṭiyā, malēciyā, cāvā, pālittīvu, pōrṉiyō kirīṭ, īṭar tīvukaḷilellām paraviya civaneṟi namatu kaṉṉiyarkaḷum āṇkaḷāṉa cāmiyārkaḷum namatu kalikaḷiṉ mūlam ulakam aṉittum civaneṟiyit taḻuvi irukkumāṟu ceytiruppārkaḷ.  yūtarkaḷukku erucalēm pōṉṟu kiṟitavarkaḷukkup petlēkam pōṉṟu, mulimkaḷukku mekkā pōṉṟu civaṉi vaḻipaṭum civarkaḷukku citamparamum, maturiyum, tirunelvēliyum kāñciyum puṉitanakarkaḷāy kōṭāṉu kōṭi cevvantiyarkaḷum nīkrōvarkaḷum, maṅkōliyarkaḷum, airōppiya veḷḷiyarkaḷum yūtarkaḷum piṟarum vantu kaḷikkum puṉita talamāka iruntirukkum.  oru āṇṭiṟku intiyāviṟku inta yāttirīkarkaḷāl 1000 kōṭi rūpāy vantu cērntu varum.
nantavaṉam
tirukkōyilkaḷil aṉṟāṭam naṭi pūcikaḷukkum tiru uruvaṅkaḷukkum tiruviḷakkukaḷukkum mālikaḷum caraṅkaḷum pantukaḷum viṭupūkkaḷum tēviyāy iruntaṉa.  puṭpa vitiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa malarkaḷ maṭṭuntāṉ kōyilkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttap
paṭavēṇṭum.  mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiliruntu nantavaṉaṅkaḷ kōyil uḷḷēyum, veḷiyēyum iruntu vantaṉa.  niṟivāṉa peruṅkōyilil kōyil kaṭṭum poḻutē tirumatilkaḷukku uṭpuṟam nantavaṉattiṟkeṉṟu taṉi iṭamum pūcceṭikaḷukku nīr pāyccuvataṟku kiṇaṟum teppamum uṇṭu paṇṇiyiruntaṉar.  nāyaṉmārkaḷil āṇkaḷ tirunāvukkaracar, campantar, cuntarar, māṇikkavācakar pōṉṟa camaya kuravarkaḷāka uyarpatavi vakitta nāyaṉmārkaḷum nantavaṉattiṟku nīrpāyccutal kōyilkaḷic cuttam ceytal mutaliya tiruppaṇi viṭikaḷit tavaṟātu ceytu vantaṉar.  tirunāvukkaracar eppoḻutum taṉatu kiyil pul cetukkum karuviyit tāṅkiyiruntār.  uḻavārap paṭi kiyiluṭiyāṉ eṉṟu patiṉōṟān tirumuṟiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  avar cellum kōyilkaḷil puṟkaḷic cetukkum tiruppaṇiyit tavaṟātu ceytuvantār.  iṉṟu cuvāmi tericaṉam ceyya ½ parlāṅku tolivu cella veḷḷip pallakkil cellum maṭātiparkaḷ itic cintittu pārkkumpaṭi paṇivāyk kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟēṉ.  namatu camayatti vaḷartta nāyaṉmārkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇiyi niṉintu avar vaḻiyil celvatē uṇmiyāṉa paktarkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākum.  āṇ makaṉāṉa nāyaṉmārkaḷum paktarkaḷum nantavaṉattiṟku nīr iṟittu pāycci vantaṉar.  kaṉṉimāṭattiluḷḷa makaḷir pūkkoytu mālikaḷum pūccaraṅkaḷum pūcceṇṭumāka palavitamākap pūkkaḷit toṭuttu iṟivaṉiyaṭiya tiruuruvaṅkaḷil cāṟṟumpaṭi ceytu vantuḷḷaṉar.  kaṭṭiṭakkali, paṭimakkali, ōviyakkali pōṉṟa 64 kalikaḷil pūttoṭuttalum oru kali.  pūt toṭukkum kaliyi kaṉṉimāṭattil vāḻum makaḷir naṉku kaṟṟiruntaṉar.  atōṭu āṭavum pāṭavum payiṉṟiruntaṉar.
nantavaṉaṅkaḷil pūcikkutavāta malarkaḷ iṭam peṟātu.  pūcikkuriya malarc ceṭikaḷi iṉavāriyāka naṭṭu nīrpāycci pūttapiṉ viṭiyaṟkālattilē avikaḷip paṟittut toṭuttu kōyilukkuc koṇṭu pōyc cērppar.  pūppaṟittu mālikaḷ kaṭṭumpaṇi mikavum ciṟanta toṇṭākak karuti pala civa vēḷāḷarp peṇkaḷ pakti mēlīṭṭāl taṅkaḷi it toṇṭiṟkut  arppaṇittuk koṇṭaṉar.  civa camayattil civakāmiyāṇṭār eṉpavarum viṇavattil periyāḻvārum ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa toṇṭar āvar.  civa camayattil uḷḷa celvamikka  uyarkulap peṇkaḷ tirumaṇa vāḻkkiyi veṟuttu  it teyvat tiruppaṇikku taṅkaḷi arppaṇit tuḷḷaṉar.  iṉṟu kattōlik kiṟitava camayattil uḷḷa kaṉṉiyarkaḷip pōl civa camayattilum kaṉṉimāṭaṅ
kaḷilum atil tiruppaṇi ceyyum peṇkaḷum ērāḷamāka iruntaṉar.  avarkaḷ eḷiya uṭiyum, eḷiya naṭiyum uṭiyavarkaḷ.  taṅkaḷ celvam aṉittiyum nantavaṉaṅkaḷi niṟuvavum kiṇaṟukaḷit tōṇṭavum māṉiyamāka aḷittuviṭṭu piṟa ēḻi eḷiya toṇṭarkaḷip pōl eḷiya uṭiyum uṇavum peṟṟu teyvat tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭu vantaṉar.  nantavaṉam niṟuvavum kiṇaṟukaḷ tōṇṭavum maṉṉarkaḷum celvarkaḷum māṉiyaṅ
kaḷum aḷippatum uṇṭu.  pūkkaḷil kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nīrppū, nilappū eṉa nāṉku vakiyākap pirikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.  
kōyilkaḷil tiruvamutu ceyvataṟku nañcey, puñcey nilaṅkaḷit tāṉam ceytataṟku pala kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭu kaḷum iṉṟu cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nilaṅkaḷil viḷiyum nellum pullum itara kūlap poruḷkaḷum nivēttiyattiṟkum tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa ūḻiyarkaḷukku piracātamākavum vaḻaṅkap
paṭṭaṉa.  eñciyavikaḷ mikak kuṟinta viliyil ēḻikaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.
kōyilkaḷukku maṉṉarkaḷum, celvarkaḷum māṉiyamāka ērāḷamāṉa nañcey, puñcey nilaṅkaḷi māṉiyamāka viṭṭatāl kōyilkaḷukku nellum ēṉiya kūlap poruḷkaḷum vaṇṭi vaṇṭiyāy vantu iṟaṅkiṉa.  kūlap poruḷkaḷ malipōl kuvintaṉa.  eṉavē kōyilkaḷukkeṉṟu kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  maturiyil uḷḷa tirumāliruñ cōliyil uḷḷa aḻakar kōyiliṉ kūlak kaḷañciyam kōyili viṭap periyatu.  vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭivatti yuṭiyatu.  aḻakāka amin tuḷḷatu.  ivvāṟē tañcāvūril uḷḷa pala kōyilkaḷiṉ kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ kōyiliviṭap periyatu.  kōyilkaḷukku āṭu māṭukaḷum avikaḷ mēyvataṟku nilaṅkaḷum nivantamāka viṭap
paṭṭiruntaṉa.  ataṉāl ney ērāḷamāka vantatu.  ney viḷak
kerikkavum kōyilil tōci, vaṭi, poṅkal, puḷiyōtari, carkkarip poṅkal, laṭṭu mutaliyavikaḷ  ceyyappaṭṭu makkaḷukkuk kuṟinta viliyil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.  iṉṟum pala kōyilkaḷil cōṟum palakāraṅkaḷum kuṟinta vilikku viṟkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  viṇavak kōyilil iṉṟum palakāraṅ
kaḷum ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum tārāḷamākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  
kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum
muṟkālattil namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ nāṭṭu varalāṟu eṉṟu taṉiyē nūl etuvum eḻutavilli.  āṉāl namatu varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ pala namatu tirukkōyilkaḷil kalveṭṭukaḷākavum ceppēṭukaḷākavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  namatu kōyilkaḷil nūṟāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ iṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kalveṭṭukaḷ kōyil cuvarkaḷilum, paṭikkaṭṭukaḷilum, makkaḷ naṭantu cellum pātikaḷilum veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ceppēṭukaḷ, ōli ēṭukaḷ pōl cemput takaṭṭāl ceyyap
paṭṭu atil araca muttirikaḷum patikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, pallavar ākiya tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku aḷitta nivantaṅkaḷi ceppēṭukaḷil eḻuti aracarkaḷ muttiriyum, eḻutiyavar peyarum, cāṭcikaḷum oppamiṭṭu, iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum.  āvaṇakkaḷariyil pativu ceyyappaṭṭa pattiram pōl aracu pativu (Register) ceytu kōyilkaḷil ceppēṭukaḷil pātukāttu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aracukaḷiṉ māṟṟattiṉālum, kālak koṭumiyālum makkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmiyālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukaḷum, ceppēṭukaḷum kavaṉiyātu maṟintu pōyviṭṭaṉa.  āṅkila āṭci nilaviyapoḻutu, aracu taṉiyāka, kalveṭṭiyal (Epigraphy Deapartment) eṉṟu oru taṉit tuṟi niṟuviṉar.  tol eḻuttiyal (Paleography) eṉṟa oru āyvut tuṟiyi amittu palvēṟu varivaṭivaṅkaḷil eḻutap peṟṟirukkum eḻuttukkaḷip paṭikka mutaṉ mutalāka āṅkila aṟiñar puḵlar, intiyat tol eḻuttiyal (Indian Paleograpy) eṉṟa oru nūli eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  ataṟku intiyā avarukku naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu.  intat tuṟi nīṇṭakālamāka āṅkila aṟiñar talimiyil nīlakiriyil iyaṅki vantatu.  appāl intiyar talimiyil micūril iyaṅki varukiṟatu.  cumār 20,000 - ttiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ paṭikkappaṭṭu, pala nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa.  intiyā urimi eytiyapiṉ ilaṭcattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kal veṭṭukaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu nūlākkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.  intiya aracō tamiḻaka aracō pōtiya akkaṟi kāṭṭavilli.  ittuṟi āmi vēkattil metuvāka iyaṅkivarukiṟatu.  āṉāl maṟintuviṭavilli.  ittuṟiyi namatu aracu naṉku ceyalpaṭac ceytiruntāl nūṟāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷum ceppēṭu
kaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu namatu nāṭṭiṉ iruṇṭa varalāṟṟiṉmītu putiya oḷi ūṭṭap peṟṟirukkum.  cintu veḷi akaḻ āyvum muttirikaḷum, ulakilē aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, tirāviṭa makkaḷum, civaṉeṟiyum, uḻavar, paravar, kuravar pōṉṟa makkaḷiṉ aracum, nākarikamum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyati eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyatu pōl, teṉintiyak kal veṭṭukaḷ tamiḻar, kēraḷar, āntirar, kaṉṉaṭar oppaṟṟa nākarikattik kāṇat tuṇiyāka iruntirukkum.
tamiḻakattil aracakaṭṭilil amarnta teṉ intiya nala urimic caṅkamō, kāṅkira āṭciyō ti.mu.ka aracō taṅkaḷ pataviyik kāppāṟṟik koḷḷa muyaṉṟatē yoḻiya ittakiya nalla tuṟiyil nāṭṭam celuttavilli.
tolleḻuttiyal
toṉmiyāṉa kālattil eḻutappeṟṟatu.  ataṉi āyum muṟi tol eḻuttiyal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  tol eḻuttu eṉpatu iṉṟu vaḻakkil iruntu varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muntiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum, vaḻakkoḻinta ataṉ paḻaṅkāla eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum ām. tol eḻuttukkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vaḷarcciyum āyvu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ toṉmiyiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyiyum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaṉ āyntu ataṉi aṉittulaka iyalāka uyarttiyuḷḷaṉar.  
makkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarccikku aṭikōliyatu eḻuttu kālattiyum, nāṭṭiyum, nākarikattiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu eḻuttu.  paṇṭik kālattil enta nāṭṭil enta iṉa makkaḷ eḻuttik kaṇṭārkaḷō avarkaḷtāṉ nākarika makkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.  eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇ eṉat takum eṉpatu auvip pirāṭṭiyāriṉ amuta moḻi.  eṇṇeṉpa ēṉi eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum kaṇ eṉpa vāḻum uyirkku eṉpatu vaḷḷuvar vākku.
ōviyaṅkaḷ
paṇṭik kālattil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tam uḷḷak karutti maṟṟavarkaḷukkup pulappaṭutta kōl koṇṭu tariyilum, maṇalilum ōviyantīṭṭi uṇarttivantaṉar.  ataṉ mūlam oruvarukkoruvar karuttup parimāṟṟam ceyyum kali vaḷarntuvantatu.  ōviyamē namatu eḻuttiṟku aṭippaṭi.  ōviya eḻuttiyē ulakil vāḻum ellā makkaḷum mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu, iṉṟu tol eḻuttiyal āyvil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  aiyāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ eḻutap payiṉṟiruntaṉar.  eḻuttu eṉṟa tamiḻppatam eḻutu eṉṟa viṉic colliṉ aṭiyākap piṟanta collākum.  eḻutu eṉṟāl vari eṉap poruḷ tarum.  nikaṇṭil eḻuttu eṉpataṟkup paṭam uruvam ōviyam eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ tam varivaṭivaṅkaḷi ōviyak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉiṉṟē kaṇṭupiṭittu ataṟku eḻuttu eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  caṅka nūṟkaḷil ōviyattiṟku eḻuttu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  muṟkālattil tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattiluḷḷa murukaṉ kōyilil oru ōviya maṇṭapam iruntatu.  ataṉik kuṉṟam pūtaṉār eṉṉum pulavar 
niṉ kuṉṟattu
eḻutoḻil ampalam kāmavēḷ ampiṉ
toḻil vīṟṟirunta nakar		     -(paripāṭal)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟku, niṉkuṉṟattu eḻutiya eḻil tarum ampalam eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar poruḷ koḷḷukiṟār.  iṅkuḷḷa cittira māṭattil cila ōviyaṅkaḷi nappaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar kaṇṭu ataṉi viḷakkik kūṟum poḻutu, 
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttu nili maṇṭapam       (paripāṭal)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  iṅku, cittira māṭatti, eḻuttu nili maṇṭapam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tamiḻakattil muṟkālattil ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ iruntaṉa.  tamiḻarkaḷ paḻaṅkālattil pala vakiyāṉa eḻuttukkaḷip payiṉṟu vantuḷḷaṉar.  yāpparuṅkala virutti ilakkaṇak kottu mutaliya nūṟkaḷ mūlam tamiḻ eḻuttu uruveḻuttu uṇarveḻuttu oli eḻuttu taṉmi eḻuttu eṉa nāṉku pirivukaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭiruntatu.
kāṇappaṭṭa vuruvamellām
māṇak kāṭṭum vakiyi nāṭi

eḻutappaṭuvatu uruveḻuttākum	
(yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōḷ) 
uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmi
iruvaki yeḻuttu mīriraṇ ṭākum

eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
camavayaṅka cutta eṉṉum camaṇanūl ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntatu.  itil 18 vitamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ippaṭṭiyalil tamiḻi eṉṟu tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  pirāmi piṟakkum poḻutē tamiḻiyum piṟantatu;  vaḻakkil iruntatu.
lalita vitāram eṉṉum nūlil 64 vaki eḻuttukkaḷ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.   itil tirāviṭi eṉṉum patamum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.  tirāviṭi eṉpatu teluṅku ,kaṉṉaṭam, kirantam ākiya eḻuttukkaḷik kuṟippiṭuvatākum.  ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ varivaṭivaṅkaḷ māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
1903 -ām āṇṭil varivaṭiva āyvāḷar tiru.veṅkōparāv mutaṉ mutalil kīḻvaḷavu eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittār.  1906 - ām āṇṭil tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri (District Collector) kemiṭu eṉṉum āṅkilēyar maṟukāl eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ iruppatik kaṇṭupiṭittār.   ataṉ piṉṉar āṇṭippaṭṭiyil īyak kācil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa.  appāl tamiḻ āciriyar araccalūril irācu eṉpavar tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittār.  appāl tiruvāta ūril ai. makātēvaṉum koṟkiyil tamiḻakat tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟiyic cērnta majittum, piṟarum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar.  maturiyic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kuṉṟu
kaḷilum, kuṟṟālattiluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷilum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tol  eḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟu, kali, paṇpāṭu, araciyal, nākarikam mutaliyavikaḷi aṟivataṟkuk kalveṭṭukaḷ aṭippaṭic cāṉṟu
kaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.  varivaṭivaṅkaḷ arumpuvataṟku muṉṉar karuttu veḷiyīṭṭiṟkāka ekiptu, cumēriyā, intiyā, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil cila muṟikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu iṉṟu tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉarum, eḻuttiyal nipuṇarkaḷum piṉvarum muṟikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatākak kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
1. kuṟiyīṭṭu moḻi (Sign Language) peruviyaṉ muṟi (Perunrium quipus) kuḻū uk F¿fŸ(Joten Signs) 
2. ōviya eḻuttumuṟi (Pictographs) ōviya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ - vaṭa amerikka intiyarkaḷiṉ vāmpum kacci (Wampum belt) kalumeṭ kuḻal (Calumet Peace Pipe) karuttup paravalē mukkiya nōkkamāy amintatu.
3. karuttup paṭak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ.  (Pidegrams)
4. col allatu eḻuttukkup patilāka niṟkum poruḷ vaṭivam (Hieroglyphs) col vēṟupāṭukaḷ, hīrō kiḷipciṉ vakikaḷ.
5. āppuk kuṟi eḻuttumuṟi (Cunciforum Writing)
6. cīṉa eḻuttumuṟi kiyāṅ ciṅ (Hsiang hsing) cih cih (Chich Shih)
ātiyil ulakameṅkum makkaḷ tam karuttit terivikka ōviyaṅkaḷiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.  intiyāvil uḷḷa mattiyap piratēcattiluḷḷa pimpaṭkār kuṉṟil kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭa pīṭuṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷ nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta mutirāp paṇpuṭiya mutumakkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyamākum.  makātēva maliyil uḷḷa maṅkā ōviyaṅkaḷum mīrcāppūril kaṇṭa ariya ōviyaṅkaḷum, takkāṇattil reyccūr māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa koppal, pikkiḷikkal, maki, mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷum, vintiya malikaḷil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷum intiyāvil eḻuttup piṟappataṟku ēṟṟa aṭippaṭiyāy iruntaṉa.
ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ
ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻuttukkaḷ ākā.  tīṭṭiya uruvi  maṭṭum ōviyam kuṟikkumēyoḻiya vēṟu etiyum kuṟikkātu.  makkaḷ malikaḷilum kāṭukaḷilum tirintalintati viṭṭu, āṟṟaṅ
karikaḷil pala makkaḷ iṇintu kuṭiyēṟi, payirt toḻili vaḷarttu nakara vāḻkki naṭattiyapoḻutu taṅkaḷ āṭu māṭu, ēṉiya poruḷkaḷik kuṟikka kuṟiyīṭukaḷi uruvākkat terintaṉar.  makkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷukku uṟiviṭamākak kōyilkaḷi uruvākkiṉar.  ūrt teyvaṅkaḷukku makkaḷ kāṇikkiyāka āṭu māṭu pōṉṟa
vaṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉar.  avikaḷ ērāḷamāka perukiṉa.  avikaḷik kaṇakkiṭap pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tēviyāyiṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kōyilkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷilō allatu kaṟkaḷilō māṭṭiṉ uruvaṅkaḷit tīṭṭi pakkattil oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu eṉpatik kāṭṭa kōṭukaḷit tīṭṭiṉar.  akkuṟikaḷ kōyilkaḷukku ettaṉi māṭukaḷ uḷa eṉpatit terivittaṉar.  kōṭukaḷ iṉṟi puḷḷikaḷum iṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  ivaṟṟi ōviya eḻuttu eṉṟaṉar.  mecapottāmiyāviṟ maṇ ōṭukaḷil ittakiya ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.  mīṉōcūṉ kiriṭē eṉṉum paḻam pakutiyilum inta eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
karutteḻuttukkaḷ
 ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcci peṟṟu karutteḻuttukkaḷākap pariṇamitteḻuntaṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ñāyiṟṟi maṭṭum kuṟikkum oru vaṭṭam ōviya eḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.  ataṉ tōṟṟam, ataṉ veppa nili mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭum kuṟikaḷ karutteḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.   iḵtaṉṟi māṭṭiṉ kālkaḷiyō kompukaḷiyō varintu māṭṭik kuṟikka payaṉpaṭuttiṉar itaṉik karuttu eḻuttu (Ideogram) eṉpar.  ōviya eḻuttilum, karutteḻuttilum iṭappaṭṭa kuṟikaḷ olikkappaṭum ataṉ peyarukkum evvita campantamum illi.
oli eḻuttukkaḷ
makkaḷ nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟapiṉ oli eḻutti uruvākkiṉar.  oru poruḷukku peyarum, ataṉ oliyi eḻuta oli eḻuttum kaṇṭaṉar.  ātiyil oru vilaṅku, allatu paṟavi, maram, vīṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟku orē eḻutti uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar.  eṭuttuk
kāṭṭāka ā=pacu, i=itu, ī=vaṇṭu, ū=ūṉ, ē=pāṇam,  ai=aracaṉ, ō=maṭi, kā=kāttal, kī=kiḷikkural, kū=pūmi, ki=karam, kō=aracaṉ, cā=cāveṉṉēvil, cī=ilakkumi, cē=civappu, cō=araṇ eṉṟu poruḷ tarum muṟiyil pala eḻuttukkaḷ uḷa.  tā, tī, tū, tē, ti, ta, tō, nā, nī, nō, pā, pū, pē, pi, pō, mā, mī, mū, mi, vā, vī, vē, vi pōṉṟaṉa. appāl aci, āci, ici, īki, uri, ūr, eli, ēṭu, jaṉam, oki, ōṭu, kaṇ, kāṭu, kiḷi, kīri, kuki, kūṭu, keṭu, kēḷ, kiti, koṭi, kōṭu, kuri pōṉṟa īreḻuttuṭiya coṟkaḷ tamiḻil eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.  iḵtaṉṟi cū, cūriyaṉ, mā-māṭu, ā-āṭu eṉṟa muṟiyil mutaṉmutalāka eḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  itē pōṉṟu poruṭkaḷi ataṉ uruvaṅkaḷi eḻutiṉar nāḷaṭivil ac coṟkaḷiṉ mutal oliyi maṭṭum kuṟikkavum toṭaṅkiṉar.  immuṟikku, mutal oli eḻuttu muṟi (Acrophony) eṉṟu peyar.  itu pōṉṟa vaḷarcci
kaḷāl oliyeḻuttukkaḷ uruppeṟṟaṉa.  oli eḻuttukkaḷ poruḷiyō karuttiyō kuṟiyātu kuṟippiṭṭa oliyiyē kuṟittaṉa.  oli eḻuttu enta moḻiyil variyappaṭṭatō am moḻiyāltāṉ ati aṟiya muṭiyum.  uruvattiṟkum olikkum toṭarpu iṉṟi iruntālum avvuru avvoliyi maṭṭum kuṟikka eḻuntatāy nili peṟṟuviṭṭatu.  inta eḻuttu oli eḻuttu (Phonatic Writing) eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟatu.
oli eḻuttu akaravaki eḻuttu,  uyir eḻuttu eṉa iruvakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭatu.  uyir eḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu eṉṟu āṅkilattil eḻuntaṉa.  tamiḻil uyir eḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu, uyirmeyeḻuttu, eṇkaḷ mutaliya niral peṟa eḻuntatu.  mikap piṟkālattil eḻunta āṅkila moḻi, tamiḻ  eḻuttip pōṉṟu oḻuṅkupeṟa amikkappaṭavilli.  tamiḻil uyir eḻuttu a, i, u, e, ai, o, ōḷa eṉṟu oḻuṅkupeṟa amikkap paṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, neṭil eḻuttum amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  āṅkila moḻiyil V(A) pi (B) ci (C) ṭi (D) <(E) eṉṟu amintirukkiṟatēyoḻiya ē, ī, ai, ō, yū (A.E.I.O.U) eṉṟu amiyavilli.  atōṭu āṅkilattil eṇ intiyāviṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ vāṅkap peṟṟuḷḷa eṇṇēyākum.
intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa cintu veḷiyil ki.mu.3000 - m āṇṭiṟkumuṉ eḻuttukkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.  avi ōviya eḻuttuk
kaḷākap piṟantaṉa.  ivi muḻuvatum ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ alla.  cila oli eḻuttukkaḷum uḷa.  ivaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ ōviya -oli eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu urippar.
cintu veḷi cittira eḻuttukkaḷē intiya eḻuttukkaḷukku tāy eḻuttukkaḷ ākum.  itiṉiṉṟu vaṭa pirāmiyum, teṉ pirāmiyum karōṣṭiyum tōṉṟiṉa.  teṉpirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  tamiḻakattil nilaviya toṉmiyāṉa eḻuttu tamiḻi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.  paṇṭiyat tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷi tamiḻi, tirāviṭi eṉṟu pirākirutamoḻiyiṉar muṟkālattil aḻittu vantaṉar.  cila aṟiñarkaḷ pirāmi tōṉṟiya poḻutē tamiḻi eḻuttukkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉṟum, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, tuḷuva eḻuttukkaḷ tirāviṭi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
kuki eḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭil kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉṉuḷḷa tamiḻk kalveṭṭukkaḷ perumpālum kukiyiṉ mēṟpakutiyil veṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu terikiṟatu.  araccalūril uḷḷa kalveṭṭu ici eḻuttukkaḷik kuṟippaṉavāy uḷḷatu.  kukik kalveṭṭukkāṉa pāṉi ōṭukaḷilum ivveḻuttukkaḷ kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa.  arikkaṉ mēṭṭil putta camayat toṭarpuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  āṇṭippaṭṭiyil kiṭitta caṅkakāla īyak kācukaḷil pirāmi vari vaṭivil tiṉṉaṉ etirāṉ cēntaṉ ā eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itu ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭic cārnta eḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  
tamiḻ eḻuttu
ki.pi. mūṉṟāṇṭiliruntu tamiḻ moḻiyi eḻuta tamiḻi eḻuttiyum, vaṭṭeḻuttiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.  itaṉi tamiḻ tol poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟi iyakkunar tiru. irā. nākacāmi kalveṭṭiyal eṉṉum nūlil teḷivāy viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  pallavarkaḷ maṉṉaṉ cimma viṣṇu kālac ceppēṭukaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttilum kiranta eḻuttilum eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
muṟkālac cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷ tamiḻil aḻakākap piḻi
kaḷiṉṟi veṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  piṟkālac cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷ atāvatu irācarācaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉṉar veṭṭiya kalveṭṭukaḷ ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭavilli.  vicaya nakara nāyakka maṉṉar kālattuk kalveṭṭukkaḷum aḻakiṉṟip piḻikaḷ malintuḷḷaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mutal pallava maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ceppēṭukaḷ teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, kiranta eḻuttukaḷil uḷḷaṉa.   pala kalveṭṭukaḷ kiranta eḻuttilum nākarika eḻuttilum uḷḷaṉa.
maturip pāṇṭiyarkaḷ ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 
9 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari eḻutiya kalveṭṭukaḷ tamiḻilum kirantattilum uḷḷaṉa.  ceppēṭukaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttil uḷḷaṉa.  cēra nāṭṭil uḷḷa pala kalveṭṭukaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttil uḷḷaṉa.  atiyarkaḷiṉ kalveṭṭukaḷ pallavar kiranta eḻuttilum vaṭṭeḻuttilum uḷḷaṉa.  kuṟṟāla nātar kōyil kalveṭṭum vaṭṭeḻuttilē uḷḷaṉa.  tiru. kōpināt rāv tiṟaṉuṭiya paṇṭiya tamiḻ eḻuttiṉiṉṟu vaṭṭeḻuttut tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kōyilkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum
paṇṭik kālattil tāḷkaḷ kiṭiyātu.  ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ellām paṉa ōlikaḷilē kūriya muṉiyiyuṭiya eḵku eḻuttāṇi koṇṭē eḻutivantaṉar.  ōliyil eḻutappaṭum ēṭukaḷ oru nūṟu āṇṭukaḷ kūṭa nilittiruppatilli.  vēṟu cila nāṭukaḷil marappaṭṭikaḷil eḻuti vittiruntaṉar.  atuvum nīṇṭa kālam nilittu niṟpatilli.  cila nāṭukaḷil elumpukaḷil kūṭa eḻuti vantaṉar.  cila nāṭkaḷil pippira eṉṉum oru vaki nāṇaṟ pullil eḻuti vantaṉar.
cila nāṭukaḷil tōlil eḻuti vantaṉar.  periya ilakkiyaṅ kaḷiyum maṟinūṟkaḷiyum 1000 -kkaṇakkāṉa tōlkaḷil eḻutum paṭi nērntatu.  cātāraṇa makkaḷ ittakiya tōl nūlkaḷi vili koṭuttu vāṅka muṭiyātiruntatu.  ituvum 100 allatu 150 āṇṭukaḷukku mēl nilittu niṟka muṭiyātiruntatu.  mutaṉ mutalāka pipiḷ eppaṭi eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ciṅkappūri liruntu veḷivantuḷḷa tamiḻ nāḷ ēṭoṉṟil paṭittēṉ.  atu, aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
accu eḻuttukkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭitta kūṭaṉpark tāṉ mutaṉ mutalākap pipiḷi acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭār.  ulakilēyē mutaṉ mutal acciṭappaṭṭa puttakamum pipiḷtāṉ.
itu lattīṉ moḻiyil acciṭap peṟṟatu.  acciṭappaṭṭa āṇṭu ki.pi. 1455.
mottam 200 piratikaḷē acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.  ovvoru piratiyilum 1282 pakkaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa.  ovvoru pakkamum 36 varikaḷiyuṭiyatāy iruntaṉa.
intap puttakaṅkaḷ kākitaṅkaḷil acciṭap peṟavilli.  tāḷkaḷ kiṭiyātu.  eṉavē āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ tōlkaḷil tāṉ vētap puttakam acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.  anta vaḻakkappaṭi pipiḷum kaṉṟukkuṭṭiyiṉ tōlil tāṉ acciṭappaṭṭatu.  itaṟkāka cumār 10,000kku mēṟpaṭṭa kaṉṟukkuṭṭikaḷ kollappaṭṭaṉa.
200 piratikaḷil 47 piratikaḷ (mutalil tōlil acciṭappaṭṭa cattiya vētākama nūliṉ paṭikaḷ)  ippoḻutu cermaṉiyil uḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cālikaḷil (Museums) irukkiṉṟaṉa.
5000 -ām āṇṭukaḷukku cumēriyāviṉ kaḷi maṇ takaṭukaḷil ciṟu kuccukaḷ koṇṭu eḻuti pala ēṭukaḷ vittuḷḷaṉar.  
ekiptu nāṭṭil ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ (Hieroghph) eṉṉum ekiptiyar kaṇṭa maṟi poruṭ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi kallil tōṇṭi vācittu vantaṉar.  5000  -māṇṭukaṭku muṉ cintu veḷiyil kallil cila uruva eḻuttukkaḷit tōṇṭiyum cemput takaṭukaḷil kūṟiya  eḻuttāṇi koṇṭum eḻuti vantaṉar.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil 2000 -m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ tirukkōyilkaḷukku viṭum māṉiyaṅkaḷi kallil eḻutiyum ceppēṭukaḷil eḻutiyum  uḷḷaṉar. ceppēṭu iṉṟiyap pattirap pativu ceyvatu pōl āvaṇak kaḷariyil pativu ceyyappeṟṟatu.  atil eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyar māṉiyam aḷitta aracaṉiṉ muttiriyum iṭappaṭṭatu.  attakiya ceppēṭukaḷ tamiḻakattil nūṟāyirattiṟku mēl uṇṭu.  avikaḷil oṉṟiṉ uruvappaṭatti ittuṭaṉ iṇittuḷḷōm.  paṭam kāṇka. akkālattil eḻutap peṟṟa kalveṭṭu cācaṉam oṉṟiṉ vācakamum ittuṭaṉ tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu kāṇka:-
namatu nāṭu mikat toṉmiyāṉatu.  innāṭṭil makkaḷ nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ iṅku vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē payirtoḻili vaḷarttum kit toḻilkaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭittum periya nakaraṅkaḷiyum, akaṉṟu nīṇṭa terukkaḷiyum mūṉṟu nāṉku ēḻu māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vīṭukaḷiyum, māḷikikaḷiyum, araṇmaṉikaḷiyum kōyil kaḷiyum amittuḷḷārkaḷ.  eḵkuppōṉṟa uṟutiyāṉa uyarnta araṇkaḷi, amittuc cīrpeṟa amittuḷḷārkaḷ.  glF

āṉāl avarkaḷ tam nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷi eḻuti
vikkavilli.  cintu veḷi makkaḷ varalāṟu avarkaḷ ciṟu
kaṟkaḷil tīṭṭiya muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ mūlam cemput takaṭukaḷil (tāyattukaḷil) eḻutiya eḻuttu mūlamāyum ōraḷavu aṟintu varukiṟōm.  tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, pattuppāṭṭu, cilappatikāram pōṉṟa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mūlamāyum namatu kōyilkaḷilum, piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭikkum kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, meykkīrttikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ
mūlam oruvāṟu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ vaṭakkē imayam vari ceṉṟu etirppaṭṭu aracarkaḷiyellām veṉṟu imayamali mītu taṅkaḷatu kuṭiyiyum, ciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum poṟittu vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  ilaṅki mītu paṭiyeṭuttapōtu tirukkōṇamali mītu tam koṭiyip poṟittu vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  irācarāca cōḻaṉ taṉ makaṉ irācēntira cōḻaṉ talimiyil kaṭaṟpaṭiyi aṉuppi kaṭāram, maliyam, ciṅkapuram, tāylāntu, kampōṭiyam cāvakamum, cirivicayam, pāli, pōṉiyō pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷiyellām veṉṟu āṅkāṅkē kōyilkaḷ eḻuppi kalveṭṭukaḷ poṟittum vantuḷḷāṉ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ piṟa nāṭṭiṉ mītu veṟṟiyīṭṭiya poḻutellām tam nāṭṭil periya kōyilkaḷ eḻuppiyum, kōyilkaḷil periya tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ amittum, teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku vākaṉaṅkaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷum, tiruviḷakkukaḷum, kuṭaṅkaḷum, vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  ataṟkāka ērāḷamāṉa poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pittaḷi ulōkaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  aracarkaḷum, aracikaḷum iḷavaracarkaḷum, amiccarkaḷum, pirapukkaḷum ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷiyum nilaṅkaḷiyum nivantamāka aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  tirukkōyilkaḷil ney viḷakku erikka āṭukaḷum, māṭukaḷum aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  avikaḷ mēyvataṟku pul niṟinta mēyccal tarikaḷum nel vayalkaḷum tāṉam aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  kōyil paṇiyāṭkaḷukkum, kōyilil āṭalum pāṭalum iyaṟṟum tēvatācikaḷukku vīṭukaḷum, vayalkaḷum vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.  ivikaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kōyil cuvarkaḷilum tariyilum uḷḷa kaṟkaḷil akkālattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷil poṟittuḷḷārkaḷ.  iṉṟiyap pattiraṅkaḷ pōṉṟu ceppēṭukaḷil avarkaḷ māṉipamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭu avaṟṟil aracamuttirikaḷum, eḻutiyavar peyarum, cāṭcikaḷum peyar kaḷum eḻuti āvaṇak kaḷaṟiyil pativu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
intak kalveṭṭukaḷ ceppēṭukaḷ, ōliccuvaṭikaḷ mūlam akkālattil vāḻnta maṉṉarkaḷ peyar avarkaḷ āṭci ceyta kālam, veṟṟi peṟṟa nāṭukaḷ, maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṉivikaḷ, makkaḷ, cēṉātipatikaḷ peyarkaḷ veṟṟi  peṟṟa nāṭukaḷ, tōlviyuṟṟa aracarkaḷ, avar maṉivi, makkaḷ, mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ viparaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa.  maṉṉarkaḷ kōyilukku vaḻaṅkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ, veṇkalam, veḷḷi, poṉ pōṉṟa ulōkaṅkaḷil ceytu koṭutta kuṭam, cempu, koppari, keṇṭi, kiṇṇam, taṭṭu, tāmpāḷam, kiviḷakku, nili viḷakku, kuttuviḷakku, kiḷi viḷakku, nantā viḷakku, tūṇ viḷakku, tūkku viḷakku, laṭca tīpam, pōṉṟavikaḷiyum nila aḷavi muṟikaḷiyum aṟiyalām.  maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyariyum paṭṭattiyum, perumiyiyum avarkaḷatu meykkīrttikaḷ mūlam aṟiyalām.
kalveṭṭukaḷ perumpālum tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ (karppak kirakattiṉ) veḷic cuvarkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭirukkum.  cila, tūṇkaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, pakkac cuvarkaḷ, paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ, tariyil patippikkappaṭṭirukkum kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil poṟikkap paṭṭirukkum.  ikkalveṭṭukaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mātiriyik koṇṭē kōyilkaḷ eḻunta kālatti oruvāṟu kaṇikka muṭiyum.
kalveṭṭukaḷ vati srī eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril toṭaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  pallavarkaḷ ceppēṭukaḷ cilavaṟṟil jitampa kavatā eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āy maṉṉaṉ aḷitta ceppēṭukaḷ cila namō nārāyaṇāya eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.  cila ōm vati srī eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.  vēṟu cila, hari naṉṟākak kuruvē tuṇi. haricupamatu hari srī kaṇapatiyē nama, civayam eṉṟum toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.  appāl koṭi aḷikkum aracaṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ purāṇa marapum varalāṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  appāl koṭi koṭukkum kālattil āṭcipurinta aracaṉ, avaṉ āṭci arumpiya āṇṭum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷil aracaṉ peyar, avaṉ āṭci toṭaṅkiya āṇṭu, avaṉatu veṟṟip piratāpam, paṭṭam mutaliyavikaḷum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mutalām irācarācaṉ kālam mutal mutaṉmutalāka aracarkaḷatu meykkīrttiyum cērkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  meykkīrttiyil aracaṉiṉ vīracceyalkaḷ, paṭṭattaracikaḷ peyar mutaliyavi iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nōkkaṅkaḷ
perumpālāṉa kalveṭṭukaḷ aracarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku nilaṅkaḷ tāṉamāka vaḻaṅkiyatum, kōyilkaḷukku nontā viḷakku aḷittu, viḷakku erikkum neykkāka cāvā mūvāp pērāṭu, pacu allatu erumi vaḻaṅkiyatik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.  nilaṅkaḷ kōyilukkō allatu taṉippaṭṭa naparkaḷukkō viṟṟāl annilattiṉ parappu ataṉ nāṟpuṟa ellikaḷ, anta nilattic cārntirukkum piṟa poruḷkaḷ ellām kuṟippiṭappaṭum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka in nāṟpāṟ ellikku uṭpaṭṭa uṇṇilam oḻiviṉṟi  uṭumpoṭi āmi tavaḻum iṭam aṉittum kīḻnōkkiya kiṇaṟum mēl nōkkiya maramum, mīṉpaṭu paḷḷamum, tēṉpaṭu potumpum puṟṟum teṟṟiyum ākiya ellām viṟkap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  atōṭu, aḷikkum koṭi cantira cūriyar uḷḷaḷavum nili peṟṟirukkum eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  iṟutiyil paṉmāhēvarar rakṣi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭu viṭṭu inta aṟakkoṭikku tīṅku niṉittālō allatu iṭi ceytālō kaṅkik kariyilē kārām pacuvik koṉṟa tōcatti aṭivārkaḷ eṉṟō. ittarmattiṟku akitam niṉittār kaṅki iṭi kumariyiṭi eḻunūṟṟuk kātattilum ceytār ceyta pāvattup paṭuvār eṉṟum ittarmat
tiṟku ātāmoruvar picaku paṇṇiṉāl civa turōkiyumāy mōvāy pukku muliyeḻuttāṉumāy oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum piṟantāṉumallāṉāy kōmāṅki catti pucippāṉumāy, kaṅki yiṭi kumariyiṭi kurāl pacu koṉṟāṉ pāvam koḷvāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kalveṭṭukaḷil aracaṉ tēvar, perumāḷ, nāyaṉār eṉṟum araci nampirāṭṭiyār eṉṟum aracakulamakaḷir āḻvār, nācciyār eṉṟellām kuṟikkap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟantupōṉa maṉṉaṉip putikkum iṭam paḷḷippaṭi eṉṟu kalveṭṭukaḷil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  aracaṉip putitta iṭattil kōyilkaḷ eḻuppum paḻakkamum akkālattil uḷḷatu.  aracaṉuṭiya āṇi, tirumantira ōli eṉṉum atikāri eḻuti iṟutiyil tirumantira ōli nāyakam eṉṟa atikāriyāl oppu nōkkappaṭukiṟatu.  piṉṉar viṭiyil atikāri vari eṉṟa ēṭṭil eḻutum paṭi āṇiyiṭuvār.  naṭuvirukki atikāri ati eḻutum paṭi ceyvār.  puravu varitiṇikkaḷam, paṭṭōli ākiya atikārikaḷ oppamiṭṭu koṭi aḷikkappaṭṭa nilam irukkum ūrukku anta āṇi eṭuttuc cellappaṭum.
mātirikkāka iṅku cila kalveṭṭukkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avi aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
irājarājaṉ kalveṭṭu
(ki.pi. 1010)
(ikkalveṭṭu tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam, tañcāvūrp peruvuṭiyār)
vati. srī. ētat vicva nrupacrēṇi muli mālōpalālitam cācaṉam rājēya rājakēsari varmaṇa:
tirumakaḷ pōlap perunilac celviyum taṉakkē urimi pūṇṭami maṉakkoḷa kāntaḷūrc cāli kalam aruttaruḷi vēṅki nāṭum kaṅka pāṭiyum taṭiki pāṭiyum nuḷampa pāṭiyum kuṭamali nāṭum, kollamum kaliṅkamum eṇtici pukaḻ tara īḻamaṇṭalamuma iraṭṭapāṭi ēḻari ilakkamum, tiṇtiṟal veṉṟit taṇṭāl koṇṭa taṉ eḻil vaḷar ūḻiyuḷ ellā yāṇṭum toḻutaka viḷaṅkum yāṇṭē, ceḻiyarit tōcukoḷ kō rāja kēcaripanmarāṉa srīrājarāja tēvarkku yāṇṭu irupat tāṟāvatu nāḷ irupatiṉāl uṭiyār srīrāja rāja tēvar tañcāvūrk kōyiliṉuḷḷāl, irumaṭi cōḻaṉiṉ kīḻit tirumañcaṉa cāli tāṉañ ceytaruḷāliruntu, pāṇṭiya kulācaṉi vaḷanāṭṭu, tañcāvūrk kūṟṟattut tañcāvūr nām eṭuppicca tirukkaṟṟaḷi srīrājarājīcvara muṭiyārkku nām kuṭuttaṉavum, akkaṉ kuṭuttaṉavum nam peṇṭukaḷ kuṭuttaṉavum, maṟṟum kuṭuttār kuṭuttaṉavum srī vimāṉattil kallilē veṭṭuka!  eṉṟu tiruvāy moḻiñcaruḷa veṭṭiṉa.  (1) yāṇṭu irupattiñcāvatu nāḷ muṉṉūṟṟirupattiraṇṭiṉāl uṭiyār srīrājarājatēvar kuṭutta srīpali eḻuntaruḷum poṉṉiṉ koḷki tēvar oruvar, āṭavallāṉ eṉṉum kallāl niṟi eṇṇūṟṟirupat toṉpatiṉ kaḻañ cēy mukkālēy mūṉṟu mañcāṭi (2) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ patmācaṉa srīpalit tālam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟi  toḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟūṟṟiṅ kaḻañcariyē nālu mañcāṭi (3) yāṇṭu irupattāṟāvatu nāḷ patiṉālil uṭiyār srīrāja tēvar kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, āṭavallāṉ, eṉṉum kallāl niṟi nāṉūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu (4) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟi nāṉūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟu nāṟkaḻañ cariyēy iraṇṭu mañcāṭi (5) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟi nāṉūṟṟu eṇpattu nāṟkaḻañ cē kāl.
iti aṭuttu, mēlum, kāḷaṅkaḷ, tiruppaḷḷit toṅkal, tavaḷac cattiram, tirukkoṟṟakkuṭi, īccōppiki, veṇcāmarakki, kāḷañci mutaliyavikaḷum,
aṇikalaṉkaḷil ciri muṭi, vīrappaṭṭam, tiruutarappantaṉam tiruvaṭikkāri, tiruppaṭṭiki, captacari, pañcacari tirukkutampi, tōṭu, irācavarttam, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, tālimaṇivaṭam, ciricantam mutaliya poṉ aṇikaḷum, 
vaṭukavāḷi, ēkavali, muttiṉ cūṭakam, tirukkāl vaṭam, mutaliya muttu aṇikaḷum, 
kaṇṭanāṇ, pullikik kaṇṭanāṇ, pācamāli, māṇikkattiṉ tāli, cirivākuvalayam, patakkam, irattiṉa vaḷiyam, irattiṉa kaṭakam, irattiṉamōtiram, navarattiṉa mōtiram, piruṣṭa kaṇṭiki mutaliya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum, 
oṭṭu vaṭṭil, kalacam, kuṭam, taṭṭam, kuṟumaṭal, kiṭāram, mutaliya veḷḷi poṉ pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷāl ceyta parikalaṅkaḷum nipantamāka aḷikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
pallavar ceppēṭu
pallava cimmavarmaṉ (ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
ikkalveṭṭu tañci māvaṭṭam tiruttuṟip pūṇṭi vaṭṭāram paḷḷaṉ kōyil eṉṟa ūril kiṭitta ceppēṭukaḷ
kō vicaya cimhavarmaṟku yāṇṭu āṟāvatu veṇkoṟṟak kōṭṭattup perunakara nāṭṭu nāṭṭār kāṇka.  namnāṭṭu amaṇ cērkki paruttikkuṉṟil vajranantik kuravarkkup paḷḷic cantamākak koṭuttōm.  tāṅkaḷum paṭāki naṭantu kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭi aṟiyōli ceytu kuṭuttuviṭutaka?  eṉṟa nāṭṭārkkut tirumukam viṭa, nāṭṭārum tirumukam kaṇṭu, toḻutu, talikku vittu paṭāki naṭantu, kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭi nāṭār viṭutta aṟiyōlip paṭikku elli;  kīḻpāl elli ēnta lēriyiṉ kīḻikkaṭaṟṟiṉ mēṟkum, naṭik kolli elli;  iṉṉum teṉpāl elli iṉṉum nilapāṭi elli, iṉṉum vilāṭaṉkuṟṟēt tattu elli;  iṉṉum mēlpāl elli.  māvēt tattuvatiyiṉ kiḻakkum, mummūli ēttattu elli iṉṉum āṟṟelli;  iṉṉum vaṭapāl elli peruṅ kolliyiṉ teṟku iṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ kayattu elli.  iṉṉum murukkaṅ kēṇi elli ivvicitta perunāṉku elliyakattum akappaṭṭa nilam, nīr nilamum puṉceyyum kaḷarum kaṉṟu mēypāḻum kāṭum pīṭilikiyum kiṭaṅkum kēṇiyum maṉiyum, maṉippaṭappum, uṭumpōṭi āmi tavaḻntatu ellām uṇṇilaṉ oḻiviṉṟi kōvum poṟiyum māṟṟu, kuṭinīkki, ivvūr peṟṟa vajra nantik kuravarkkē paḷḷic cantamāka tāmarilum patiṉāṟarippaṭṭi nilam koṭuttōm innilattiṟkelli;  kīḻpāl elli pulikiḻār paṭṭiyiṉ mēṟkum teṉpāl elli muḷḷi pāttelliyiṉ vaṭakkum mēlpāl elli pōkkuvāyc ceṟuviṉ kiḻakkum, vaṭapāl elli pallāṅkuḷik kāliṉ teṟkum, innāṉku elliyakattum akappaṭṭa pūmiyum paruttik kuṉṟiṉ vajra nantik kuravarkkē paḷḷic cantamāka paratatti ceṉṟatu, narapayaṉ kaṇattiyāl sittam.
irāmanātapuram kalveṭṭu
(paḻam pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ māṟaṉ caṭiyaṉ)
(ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
1. srī ko māṟañcaṭiyaṉoṭu cōḻa nāṭṭiṭavi yāt
2. tiri ceyta parāntakap paḷḷa veḷāṉākiṉa nak
3. kam puḷḷaṉ ṟaṉ perāṟ puḷḷaṉeri eṉṟu
4. kuḷa mākkik kaṟkotik kumuḻi ceyvittuk kuṟi
5. ppaṇi niṉṟatu muṟṟup peṟuttāṉ puḷḷana
6. kkaṉ ṉatuceyta taccaṉ vaṭukaṅ kūṟṟaṉ a
7. vaṉ makaṉ kuṟip paṇi muṟṟuvikka puḷḷa na
8. kkaṉ avaṉukkuk kāṇiyāka aṭṭiṉa pūmip paṉ
9. ṉi nāṭ ṭiraṇṭu kūṟṟilum ūrkūḷattuk kīḻ
10. tarila nīr pāṭu kāl loro vayal patak
11. ku nel
irāmanātapuram tiṇṭukkal tālukāvil uḷḷatu.  tiṇṭukkal vakiyaṟāl cakkāmpaṭṭi jamīṉtār cēkaram eṉṟu itu makkeṉciyiṉ piratiyil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ūrukku vaṭapuṟam mantiyiṉ paṭapāṟiyiṉ mēl cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  (eṇ 600 of 1905) itu 10 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻ kalveṭṭu, itu - pāṇṭiyaṉ māṟaṉ caṭiyaṉ āṭciyil veṭṭappaṭṭatu.  aracaṉ cōḻanāṭṭic cērnta iṭaviyiṉ mītu naṭattiya irāṇuvat tākkutaliṉ poḻutu variyappaṭṭa kalveṭṭākum.
parāntakap paḷḷi vēlaṉ eṉṟa nakkaṉ puḷḷaṉ;  aracaṉ māṟaṉ caṭiyaṉōṭu, cōḻanāṭṭiluḷḷa iṭavay mīta eṭutta paṭi
yeṭuppil kalantu koṇṭāṉ.  ivaṉ taṉ peyarāl puḷḷaṉēri kuḷatti veṭṭiṉāṉ.  kaṟkaḷ patippittāṉ.  ūṟṟit tōṇṭiṉāṉ.  āṉāl avi niṟivu peṟavilli.  āṉāl avaṉ makaṉ puḷḷa nakkaṉ eṉpavaṉāl niṟivu ceyyap peṟṟatu.  kaltaccaṉ vaṭukkaṉ kuṟṟaṉ eṉpavaṉ antap paṇiyic ceytu muṭittāṉ.  puḷḷaṉ nakkaṉ avaṉukkuk kāṇiyāka paṇṇi nāṭu paṭakkunāṭu eṉṟa irupakutikaḷilum vayalkaḷ aḷittu intak kuḷattiṉ talimi paṇiyiliruntu kālvāymūlam nīrpāyccap peṟṟatu.
muṟkālattil aracarkaḷ, tamiḻ eḻuttilum (vaṭṭeḻut tilum) kiranta eḻuttilum, pirāmi eḻuttilum, vaṭamoḻi eḻuttilum mukkiyamāṉa aracu āṇikaḷi ōlikaḷil eḻuti vantārkaḷ.  ataṉ mītu araca muttirikaḷ iṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  piṉṉar nīṇṭa nāḷ irukkavēṇṭiya pattiraṅkaḷi ceppēṭu
kaḷilum kalveṭṭukaḷilum eḻutappaṭṭuvantaṉa.  pala ceppēṭukaḷi aṟiyāmiyāl aḻittuviṭṭārkaḷ.  pala leyṭaṉ, piriṭṭaṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil pātukāttu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cilar kōyil cuvarkaḷilum, paṭikaḷilumuḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷ, kōyilkaḷi iṭittuk kaṭṭum poḻutu ataṉ arumi teriyātu putittum aḻittum viṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
kalveṭṭukkaḷi, kallil cetukkumuṉ miyāl kallil eḻuti, piṉṉar uḷikoṇṭu cetukkuvārkaḷ.  mutalil civappu miyāl eḻutuvatu marapu.  cila kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ civappu mi koṇṭu eḻuti eḻuttukkaḷ veṭṭat toṭaṅki niṟivu peṟāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.  civappu miyāl eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ cila veṭṭappaṭṭum cila veṭṭap paṭāmalum irukkiṉṟaṉa.  civappu mi eḻuttukkaḷ 700, 800 - āṇṭukaḷukku mēlākiyum iṉṉum maḻiyum, veyilum, kāṟṟum tākkiyum civappu mi maṅkātu maṟiyātu puttam putitu pōl tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ippoḻutu orulaṭcattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.  cumār 25 - āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭu
kaḷum, ceppēṭukaḷum paṭiyeṭuttu vācikkap peṟṟu nūlvaṭivam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ aṟiñar mekkaṉci  eṉṉum āṅkilēyar pala kalveṭṭukaḷiyum, ceppēṭukaḷiyum tēṭi tokuttu āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavikaḷi nūlakattil pātukāttu vittuḷḷār.
mutaṉ mutalāka 1892il āṅkilattil teṉ intiya cācaṉaṅkaḷ eṉṉum peyarāl ṭākṭar i. halṭc pi.eyc. ṭi. (Dr. E. Hultzsch Ph. D) avarkaḷ pala pakutikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyam pōṉṟavarkaḷ aritiṉ muyaṉṟu palacācaṉaṅkaḷic cēkarittup paṭittu nūl ākkam ceytuḷḷārkaḷ.  (South Indian Temple Inscriprtions) 1929. putuk kōṭṭi māvaṭṭac cācaṉaṅkaḷum mūlam paṭikkappaṭṭu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  tiru. ci. civarāmamūrtti iṇṭiyaṉ epikirāpi aṇṭ cavut iṇṭiyaṉ kiripṭ eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlamākavum cila kalveṭṭukaḷ paṭikkappaṭṭu 1966 - il nūl vaṭivam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nantavaṉam
aṉṟāṭam naṭipeṟum pūcikaḷukkum tiruviḻākkaḷukkum malarkaḷ tēvippaṭuvatāl, puṣpavitiyil niyamikkappaṭṭa malarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku kiṭippataṟkāka nantavaṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiliruntē tamiḻakattil ālayaṅkaḷukkāka nantavaṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  nantavaṉaṅkaḷil pūcikku ākā malarkaḷ iṭam peṟā.  pūcikkuriya malarc ceṭikaḷi variciyāka naṭṭi nīrpāycci pūtta malarkaḷi viṭiyaṟ kālattilē paṟittu toṭuttuk kōyilukkuc cērppikkap paṭum.  pūp paṟittu mālikaḷ kaṭṭum paṇi mikavum ciṟanta toṇṭākak karutip pala peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷi ip paṇikku arppaṇittuḷḷaṉar.  civattil camayattil civakāmiyāṇṭār eṉpavarum viṇavattil periyāḻvārum ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa toṇṭarkaḷākum.  pūppaṟittu māli toṭuttalum tirukkōyil meḻukutal, kōlam pōṭutal, tēvāram pāṭutal naṭaṉamāṭutal eṉṟa teyvap paṇikku pala uyarkulap peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷi arppaṇittu iṉṟu kattōlikka camayattiluḷḷa kaṉṉiyarkaḷ pōl iruntu vantaṉar.  āṇkaḷilum ivvaṟappaṇikkut taṅkaḷi arppaṇittuk koṇṭa aṭiyavarkaḷ palar uṇṭu.  nantavaṉaṅkaḷi niṟuvi kiṇaṟukaḷit tōṇṭi taṇṇīr pāycci malar paṟitta māli toṭukkum tiruppaṇikku eṉṟum tavaṟātu naṭipeṟuvataṟku vēṇṭiya nilaṅkaḷ māṉiyam aḷitta aracarkaḷum, vaṇikarkaḷum uṇṭu.  pūkkaḷ kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nirppū, nilappū eṉa nāṉku vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  ivikaḷukku taṇṇīr pāyccu
vataṟkuk kēṇikaḷum taṭākaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭutta paṭṭiruntaṉa.  cila nantavaṉaṅkaḷ mā, palā, vāḻi, teṉṉi, kamuku mutaliya maraṅkaḷi vittu vaḷarttumvaruvar.  kōyilkaḷil tiruvamutu ceytavaṟku naṉcey, puṉcey nilaṅkaḷit tāṉam ceytatāka kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum cāṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nilaṅkaḷil viḷiyum nellum itara tāṉaṅkaḷum nivēttiyaṅ
kaḷukkum tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa ūḻiyarkaḷiṉ piracātaṅ
kaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tāṉiyaṅkaḷic cēkarittu vikkap periya kaḷañciyaṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭuvar.  tirukkōyilkaḷil nivēttiyaṅkaḷ tayār ceyya maṭappaḷḷi kaḷum uḷa.


inta atikāram eḻuta 
ātaravāka irunta nūṟkaḷ
1. pāṇṭiyaṉ ceppēṭukaḷ pattu : tamiḻ varalāṟṟuk kaḻakam ceṉṉi mutaṟpatippu 1967.
2. kalveṭṭiyal āciriyar: irā. nākacāmi, naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ maṟṟumiruvar ceṉṉi 1972.
3. ceṅkam naṭukaṟkaḷ - irā. nākacāmi, ceṉṉi 1974
4. cōḻaṉ pūrva paṭṭayam: ceṉṉi aracuk kīḻtticik kaḻaka veḷiyīṭu eṇ V 1950
5. kalveṭṭu viḷakka uri - vittuvāṉ vi. cuntarēca vāṇṭiyār uriyuṭaṉ kaṭalūr 1955
6. cācaṉa māli  veḷiyiṭṭavar e. rājam ceṉṉi 1966
7. ilakkiya ārāycciyum kalveṭṭukkaḷum - catāciva paṇṭārattār ceṉṉi 1961
8. South  Indian Inscriptions Vol  I (Tamil) Edited  Translated by E.HULTZS CH. Ph. D.  South Indian inscriptions.Vol II

ālayattiṉ tattuva viḷakkam
kōyiliṉ tattuvam
kōyil uṭampi virittuk kuṟippiṭuvatāl carīrap piratāram eṉappaṭum.  irutayatti virittuk kuṟippiṭuvatāl irutayap piratāram eṉappaṭum.  ivviruvakippaṭṭa kōyilil uṭampiṉ pañcakōcam mutalāṉavikaḷik kāṭṭuvatu eṉṟum uḷḷattil collum poruḷumāyuḷḷa vitattit kuṟippittuk kāṭṭuvatum eṉak kōyilkaḷi .iruvitamāka namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.
tattuva nūṟkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
evvuyirum parāparaṉ cannitiyatākum
	ilaṅku muyir uṭalaṉittum īcaṉkōyil
eṉṟum 
uḷḷam peruṅkōyil ūṉuṭam pērālayam
kaḷḷap pulaṉintum kāḷā maṇiviḷakkē
eṉṟum, 
kuṟikaḷum aṭiyāḷamuṅ kōyilum
neṟikaḷu malar niṉṟatōr nērmiyum
eṉṟum tattuva ñāṉikaḷ ālayatti iṟivaṉ uṟiyum kōyilākavum uyiri avaṉatu cannitiyākavum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kōyilil karppak kiraham, arttamaṇṭapam, makā maṇṭapam, nāṉa maṇṭapam, alaṅkāra maṇṭapam, capā maṇṭapam eṉṉum āṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷum uṭampiluḷḷa, 1 mūlātāram,  2 cuvātiṭṭāṉam, 3 maṇipūrakam, 4 aṉākatam, 5. vicutti, 6 ākñi eṉṉum āṟu ātāraṅkaḷiyum uṇarttuvatākum.
ālayattil aintu pirakāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  avikaḷil mutalām āvaraṇam kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭum aṉṉamaya kōcattiyum 2ām āvaraṇam kaṇmēntiriyaṅkaḷum pirāṇaṉuṅ kūṭi niṟpatākiya pirāṇamaya kōcattiyum mūṉṟām āvaraṇam ñāṉēntiriyaṅ kaḷum maṉatuṅ kūṭiyuḷḷa maṉōmaya kōcattiyum nālām āvaraṇam ñāṉēntiriyaṅkaḷum puttiyuṅkūṭiyuḷḷa viññāṉa maya kōcattiyum aintām āvaraṇam antakaraṇa viruttiyum āṉantamum kūṭiya āṉantamaya kōcattiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.  
ēḻu pirakāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  eṉil avikaḷ tōl, irattam, māmicam, mēti elumpu, macci, cukkilam eṉṉum eḻuvakit tātukkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatik kāṇka.  katavukaḷ mūṭuṅkāl civaliṅkat tirumēṉiyi maṟippatālum tiṟakkuṅkāl civatericaṉam nikaḻtalāṉum atupōl kōpam, āci āṇavam eṉpaṉa maṟikkil āṉmaliṅkam kāṇamuṭiyātu pōtaliṉ avittiyōṭu avi nīṅkiyaviṭattu āṉmaliṅkaṅ kāṇap
paṭalāṉum maṟippiyum tiṟappiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
irācacapi, tēvacapi, nirutta capi, kaṉakacapi, ciṟcapi pōṉṟa capikaḷ pirāṇamayam maṉōmayam viññāṉamayam, āṉantamayam eṉṉum 5 kōcaṅkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tūpi allatu kumpam itu irutaya kamalamāy viḷaṅkuvatu itaṉuṭiya kīḻiṭam pirutivita tattuvam, mēl nāḷam aṉpu mutalāṉa irupattumūṉṟu tattuvaṅkaḷām;  nāḷattiluḷḷa muṭkaḷ amarēcaṉ mutal cikētaṉ īṟākiya eḻupattiraṇṭu puvaṉaṅkaḷāy, eṭṭu itaḻkaḷ eṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷām aṟupattu nāṉkāyuḷḷaṉa kēcara vaṭivaṅkaḷām.  īcuram catācivam eṉṉum iraṇṭu tattuvaṅkaḷ nāḷamām pū eṉpavaṟṟiṉ nuṉi aṭimuṭiccu ākiyavikaḷ māyiyām.  pokuṭṭu caktiyām.  appokuṭṭil tōṉṟum aimpattoru vaṭivam civa tattuvaṅkaḷām.  kalacaṅkaḷ cattikaḷām.  itaṉāl irutaya kamalattil paramacivaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟa uṇmi kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.
uṭampiṉ piramarantira mattiya kapālattuvāramē uttara kōpuravāyilām.  kāṟperuviraliṉ muṭipum kiyiṉ naṭuviralkaḷiṉ muṭipum kutattuvāramum takkaṇakōpura vāyilām.  puruvamattiyamē antarāḷa kōpura vāyilām uttiyē palipiṭamām.  irutayam tuvacat tampam, pañcakōcam antak karaṇam puruṭaṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭam nanti eṉṉum aṟivar civayōkikaḷ.
tuvacat tampam cumumuṉānāṭiyām iṭikali piṅkali nāṭikaḷ itiṟceṉṟu acivaṟṟut tampam pōl niṟkun taṉmi varutaliṉ ataṟkoppāyiṟṟu iṇṭalicatti pōṉṟatu.  tuvacattampam eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar .
iti, 
āyapatitāṉ aruṭ civaliṅkam
āya pacuvu maṭalēṟeṉa niṟkum
āya palipīṭa mākunaṟ pācamām
āya varaṉili yāyntu koḷvārkkē
eṉpataṉāṟ ṟeḷika.
tiruvāci piraṇava vaṭivamām.  itaṉāl aṉittum piraṇavattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi oṭuṅkum eṉpatum, maṟṟum piraṇavam ellāt tēvarkaḷukkum piṟappiṭa māyum paracivattiṟku iruppiṭa māyum uḷḷa teṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
iti,
ōṅkāramē naṟṟiruvāci yuṟṟataṉi
ṉīṅkā veḻuttē niṟi cuṭarā - māṅkān
maṟṟā raṟivaraṇi yampalattā ṉāṭalatu
peṟṟār piṟappaṟṟār piṉ
eṉpataṉālum piṟavāṟṟāṉum uṇarka.
kōyilil tiruvalakiṭal maṇattiṉpāluḷḷa kāmam vekuḷi mutalāṉa kuppikaḷiyum toṇṇūṟṟāṟu tattuvaṅkaḷeṉṉum kuppikaḷiyum kaḷitalik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
iti, 
kāmam vekuḷi kaḻiperum poyyeṉum
tūymiyil kuppi toliviṉṟik kiṭantati
yātikiḻntu pōkki
eṉpataṉālum
poyttuṟu kuppi kiḷin toṇi poṅku
mutti yaḷitteṉa mutti taṉakku
eṉpataṉālum piṟavāṟṟāṉām uṇarka.
tiru meḻukkiṭal mēṟkūṟip pōntavāṟē kuppikaḷik kaḷinta uṭaṉ tattuva ñāṉameṉum naṟuñ cāṇattik koṇṭu aṉpeṉṉum nīruṭaṉ kalantu meḻukil, maṉōlayan tikaḻumiṭattu takarācūravoḷiyākiya civaliṅkam iṉitu viḷaṅkum eṉpatik kuṟikkiṟatu. 
iti,
ciṉa mutaliya mayaltīra māṟṟupu
 viṉitapu vāymi meḻukki yaṉpeṉum
puṉaloṭu viraviyē pūci ṉallatu
maṉameṉu maṉiyiṭi varāti liṅkamē
eṉpataṉālum,
porutiṟa ṉamiyiruḷ niṟattu
matamuṭi yaṭuciṉatti vakikkaṭāvum
yāppaviḻt takaṟṟi yampukoṭu meḻuki
eṉpataṉālum, piṟavāṟṟāṉu muṇarka.
tiruviḷakkiṭal maṉam eṉṉum takaḻiyil vivēka meṉṉum neyyi vārttu uyir eṉṉum tiriyip pōṭṭu pirāṇaṉākiya kāṟṟi niṟutti āṇavamākiya iruḷi nīkki civañāṉa oḷiyip parappuntaṉmiyik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
uṭampeṉu maṉiyakattuḷ uḷḷamē takaḻiyāka
maṭampaṭu muṇar neyyaṭṭi yuyireṉun tirimayakki
yiṭampaṭu ñāṉattīyāl erikoḷaviruntu nōkkiṟ
kaṭampamar kāḷitāti kaḻalaṭi kāṇalāmē
eṉavum ñāṉa viḷakkiṉi yēṟṟi eṉavum aruḷ viḷakkukaḷēṟṟit tupi viruḷit tuṟantu muṉpuṟa meyyeṉum vitāṉam virittu eṉavum piṟavum kūṟumāṟṟāṉaṟika.
tiric cīlipōl, māyi eṉṉum tiriyē civatti aṟiya voṭṭāmal maṟit tirukkiṉṟa teṉṟuṇarntu, ataṉi amamāyāviyākiya paramacivaṉatu tiruvaruḷāl (vāḷāl) kiḻitteṟintu viṭṭu ñāṉak kaṇṇāl civamākiya iliṅkatti nāṭi nōkkal vēṇṭum eṉpatik kuṟikkiṉṟatu tiriccili.
iti, māyap paṭalaṅkīṟit tūya ñāṉa naṭaṉam peṟṟaṉaḷ eṉavum māyit tiriyi nīkki  niṉṉi yāraṟiya vallār eṉavum piṟar kūṟumāṟṟāṉaṟika.
itaṉāṟ pōnta poruḷ eṉṉiyō veṉiṉ?  tēvālayaṅkaḷi tēvatikaḷākavē pāvittu vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum eṉpatām.  eṉavē civālayatti civamākavē cintittut taricittu vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum eṉpatu koḷḷak kiṭantami kāṇka..
aṟṟākaliṉṟē, ālayantāṉum araṉeṇat toḻumē1 eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭukiṉṟatu civañāṉa pōtta tirumaṟiyum.
 eṉavē kōyil eṉpatu uṭal eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum.  kōyil iṟivaṉ uṟiyum illattiṉ puṟacciṉṉamāka eṇṇap paṭum.  puṟac ciṉṉatti uṭal amippiṟkum ataṉ tattuvattiṟkum ēṟpa amikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻ ākamam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirumantiram uṭali naṭamāṭum kōyil eṉṟu nayampaṭa urikkiṉṟāṉ.
paṭamāṭak kōyiṟ pakavark koṉṟīyil
naṭamāṭak kōyil nampark kaṅkākā
naṭamāṭak kōyil nampark koṉṟīyil
paṭamāṭak kōyiṟ pakavark kāmē
civa camaya tattuvam āṟāniliyum piṇṭavaṭiviṟ kāṭṭum paṭam pōl viḷaṅkuvatu tirukkōyil.  itu niṉṟu payaṉ aḷikkum aruḷ niliyam.  civaṉi māṟā niṉivuṭaṉ yāṇṭum iyaṅkum makkaḷiṉ uṭal naṭamāṭum nalla tirukkōyil avartam maṟavar niṉiviṟ kuriya iṭaṅkaḷ eṭṭu.  avi niṟṟal, iruttal, kiṭattal, naṭattal, mellal, tuyilal, viḻittal, imittal eṉpaṉa.  makkaḷ uṭal naṭamāṭum kōyil eṉṟu payaṉaruḷum ciṟappuṭittatu.  it tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum ciṟappu muḻuvatum paṭam pōṉṟa inta illil irukkum paramaṉic cārum.  āṉāl paṭamāṭak kōyil vaḻipāṭu āṇṭē taṅki naṭamāṭakkōyili naṇṇuvatilli.  iviyiraṇṭum muṟiyē kal eḻuttum karuttu eḻuttum ākum.  itaṉ karuttu māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya kōyilāṉatu tattuvaṅkaḷik kāṭṭum paṭam pōṉṟa illil irukkum.  ātāraṅkaḷākiya māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya naṭamāṭum uṭampāṉa kōyil irukkum.
ciṟpa ācāriyar
peruṅkōyilkaḷiyum vilimatikka oṇṇāta kaṟ cilikaḷiyum, veṅkalap paṭimaṅkaḷiyum paṭitta ciṟpa vallunarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkaḷāl tapatikaḷ (tapatikaḷ) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar.
ciṟpa ācāriyarkaḷil poṟkollarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, marattaccarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, irumpuvēliceyyum kollarkaḷ eṉṟu aintu pirivukaḷ uṇṭu.  ivarkaḷil kōyilkaḷ, cilikaḷ veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyum taṭṭārkaḷ ciṟpikaḷ patikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.  ivarkaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ payiṉṟavarkaḷ.  ākamaṅkaḷiṉ nuṇukkamum aṟintavarkaḷ.
ivarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ;  paṇṭiyat tamiḻnāṭṭil makkaḷ, avarkaḷ vāḻum nilam paṟṟi, kuṟiñci nilamākkaḷ, mulli nilimākkaḷ, marutanilamākkaḷ, neyta nilamākkaḷ eṉṟu nāṉila mākkaḷāka vakukkap peṟṟiruntaṉar.  marutanilattil, ellā makkaḷukkum iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavu uṟpatti unti eḻuntatu.  ātiyil innilamakkaḷ kallāl nilattik kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi payirttoḻil ceytuvantaṉar.  innilattil irumpu kiṭittatu.  ataṉ payaṉāka pala irumpuk karuvikaḷ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  uḷi, cuttiyal, vāḷ, tamar ūci, arivāḷ, kōṭari, maṇveṭṭi, iḻippuḷi pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  irumpuk karuvikaḷ eḻuntatiṉ payaṉāka uḻavut toḻil vaḷarntu vaḷam peṟṟaṉa.  arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, kalappi, kōṭari mutaliya uḻutoḻil ceytavaṟkuriya irumpu vēli ceyyum kollar toḻilum maravēli ceyyum taccar toḻilum eḻuntatu.  makkaḷ uḻutal ceyvataṟku, māṭukaḷip paḻakkiṉar.  māṭukaḷ uḻuvataṟkum, maram aṭikkavum, pōr aṭikkavum, poti cumakkavum, vaṇṭi iḻukkavum, vākaṉamākavum payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.  nāḷaṭivil cempu, pittaḷi, veḷḷi, ulōkaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.  ātiyil kaḷimaṇṇāl pāṉikaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum, kutilkaḷum ceṅkalkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  appāl teyva uruvaṅkaḷum icik karuvikaḷāṉa mattaḷamum, iṭi curuṅkiya paṟikaḷum ceṅkalkaḷum cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu nīṇṭanāḷ nilittu niṟka vaḻikāṇappaṭṭatu.  nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyāy veṇkala pāttiraṅkaḷum, kal maravikaḷum, kal caṭṭikaḷum, kal viḷakkukaḷumāka kaṉṉār toḻilum, kal taccar toḻilum eḻuntaṉa.  uṇavup poruḷ
kaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, veṅkala pāttiraṅkaḷum, marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa maravikaḷum, viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷum, ulōkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa uṇṇum pāttiraṅkaḷum, uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyum nīriyum cēmittu vikkum vaṭṭil, cempu, koppari, aṇṭā mutaliya pāttiraṅkaḷum kalliṉāl ceyyap
paṭṭa pāṉi, caṭṭi, viḷakkukaḷum ceyyappaṭṭatāl, vaṇikam perukiṉa.  piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷukkum, maruta nila
makkaḷukkup paṇṭa māṟṟāka nel, eḷ, kampu, kēḷvaraku, payaṟu, uḷuntu, mutaliya uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyum, arivāḷ, kōṭari, pāṉi, caṭṭi pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷi viṟṟu vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar.  itaṉpiṉ maruta nilam vaḷam peṟṟu eḻuntatu.  aṅku māṭa
māḷikikaḷum kūṭa kōpuramum, kōyilum, araṇmaṉiyum tōṉṟiyatu.  eṅkum ciṟṟūrkaḷum, nakaramum, paṭṭiṉamum eḻuntaṉa.  kaṭal kaṭantu toḻilāṟṟum vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar.  nilam celvac ceḻippuṟṟatu.  kit toḻilkaḷ pala tuḷirttaṉa.  maruta nilattil pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ perukiṉar.
toḻil vaḷarcciyiṉ payaṉāy maruta nilam poruḷātāra vaḷam peṟṟatu.  aṅku aṟivu vaḷarcci eḻuntatu.  kalvi tuḷirttatu.  kali vaḷarntatu.  camayam tōṉṟiyatu cāttiraṅkaḷ malarntatu.  iciyum, eḻuttum piṟantatu.  aḻakuṇarcci arumpiyatu.  teyvaṅkaḷ tiruviḷiyāṭal purintaṉa.
poruḷātārac ceḻippiṉ payaṉāy kalikaḷ kaṇakkilātu kiḷarnteḻuntaṉa.  ciṟpattiṟaṉ mikka vallunarkaḷ palar tōṉṟiṉar.  maṇ, kal, cuṇṇam, maram, ulōkam, tantam mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl eṇṇaṟṟa kiviṉip poruḷkaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.  cempu, poṉ, veḷḷi, pittaḷi, veṇkalam, īyam, nākam mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷāl pala teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ vārppaṭattu vārttum uḻi koṇṭu cetukkiyum aḻakuṟac ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  pala uyir ōviyaṅkaḷ uruppeṟṟeḻuntaṉa.  teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ tiṟampaṭac ceytamiyālum, aruḷ poliyum tirukkōyilkaḷ pala āṇṭavaṉukku amittatiṉ payaṉākavum ivarkaḷ teyva kammiyar eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar.  mayaṉ, vicuvakarmā pōṉṟa teyva kammiyarkaḷ ivarkaḷ marapil tōṉṟiṉar.
ivarkaḷil maṇṇāṟ pāvikaḷic ceypavarkaḷ maṇṇīṭṭāḷar eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar.  tātup poruḷ kaḷiṉāl paṭimaṅkaḷiyum kalliṉāṟcilikaḷiyum, ceṅkalli ṉālum, karuṅkalliṉālum, paḷiṅkuk kaṟkaḷālum, veṇmi yāṉa cutikaḷiṉālum, maraṅkaḷiṉālum, tiruuruvaṅkaḷiyum, tirukkōyilkaḷiyum camippavarkaḷ kammiyar, ciṟpikaḷ, tapatikaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar.  kaṭṭiṭak kali amippil tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa ciṟpikaḷ paṇṭu  toṭṭu ulakilē oppuvamiyaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām.  cintu veḷiyil iṟṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭṭu muṉṉar periya nakaraṅkaḷiyum, makōṉṉatamāṉa māperum māḷikikaḷiyum, ulakilē uyarnta upparikkikaḷiyum, mūṭucākkaṭikaḷiyum, ulakam kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta cey kuḷaṅkaḷiyum eṭuppittavarkaḷ tamiḻakattuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷēyāvar.  vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷēyāvar.  avarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatāl vēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ ākiviṭamuṭiyātu.  tirāviṭa camūkattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ tamiḻar eṉṟu varalāṟṟup pulami  mikka aṟiñarkaḷ colvatiṉ mūlam cintu veḷi nākarikattiyum aṅku kāṇappeṟṟa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiyum kōyilkaḷiyum, kuḷattiyum civaṉ, civaliṅkam, umātēvi, āṭavallāṉ, nilatteyvam, nīrt teyvam pōṉṟa tiruuruvaṅkaḷiyum paṭittavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyam illi.
ulakilē uyarnta nākarikamāka cumār 4000 m āṇṭiṟku muṉ eḻunta ekiptiya nākarikamum, pāpilōṉ nākarikamum, kirīṭ nākarikamum tirāviṭa ciṟpat toṭarṭiyatu eṉṟum, cintu veḷi nākarikatti aḷitta āriya maṟavarkaḷē ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, kirīṭ  nākarikattiyum aḷittārkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟu pala varalāṟṟup pulavarkaḷ kūṟuvatōṭu tamiḻarkaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ teyvamākiya civaṉum, civaneṟiyum kaliyum orukālattil ulakam muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku cāṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  iṉṟillā viṭiṉum virivil ulakil uyarnta kaṭṭiṭak kalikku aṭikōliyavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ eṉṟu ulaka varalāṟṟilum kaṭṭiṭakkali varalāṟṟilum mutal iṭattil poṉ eḻuttāl eḻutappaṭum eṉṟu nampa iṭam uṇṭu.
iṉṟu, ulakil, cintu veḷi nākarikatti uruvākkiya ciṟpikaḷiṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟik koḷḷum ēṟṟam uṭiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkupavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷēyākum.  avarkaḷ amitta tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyil vimāṉamum, tiruk kaṭampūr karakkōyilum, peṇṇāṭakam tūṅkāṉi māṭak kōyilum, āvuṭiyār kōyil koṭuṅkiyum, tiruvīḻilmiḻali vuvāl nattu maṇṭapamum, citamparattil āṭum  āṭavallāṉiṉ aḻaku uruvap paṭimum, kōnērirāyapuram aimpoṉṉāṟ ceyta periya ampalak kūttaṉ paṭimamum, māmallapuram māmallapurattil uḷḷa eṇṇilā oṟṟikkal tēr uruvaṅkaḷum, (mātirik kōyilkaḷum) maturi, tirunelvēli, kiruṣṇāpuram āḷvārtirunakari, cucīntiram kōyilil uḷḷa cilikaḷum icit tūṇkaḷum, kuḻal tūṇkaḷum, aṇiveṭṭukkālkaḷum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilil iruntu, cōḻarkaḷum, pāṇṭiyarkaḷum, pallavarkaḷum, cērarkaḷum, vicaya nakara nāyakkamaṉṉarkaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil pala kaṟkōyilkaḷi eḻuppi tamiḻ nāṭṭu ciṟpikaḷiṉ kivaṇṇattik kāṭṭum cantarppatti aḷittuḷḷārkaḷ.  ataṉ piṉṉar nāṭṭil eḻunta āṭci yāḷarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu ciṟpikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉik kāṭṭum vaḻikaḷil kōyilkaḷō māḷikikaḷō amikkavilli.  ataṉāl ciṟpat toḻil kuṉṟiviṭṭatu.  ciṟpikaḷ vēṟu toḻilkaḷil īṭu paṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  ciṟpak kali vaḷarātu pōyviṭṭatu.
ariya kali
tañci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa āviṭiyār kōyil koṭuṅkiyil oru tāḷiṉ (kākitaṉ) parumaṉ irukkumāṟu kalli melliyatāka, nuṇukkamākat tamiḻakac ciṟpikaḷ cetukki irukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  kalli meṉmiyāṉa ulōkat takaṭu pōl iḻikkum nuḻipulam vāynta nuṇṇaṟivum paṭṭaṟivum, kivaṇṇamum meyvarap peṟṟavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ eṉpataṟku āviṭiyār kōyil kaṟpaṇikaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa.
tañci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tiruvīḻi miḻaliyil uḷḷa vuvāl nattu maṇṭapattil uḷḷa periya vaḷivukaḷi amittu māperum maṇimaṇṭapatti kaṭṭi muṭitta makattāṉa ciṟpa vallunarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta poṟiyiyal aṟivu eṉṟum pōṟṟattakkatu eṉṟu ciṟpak kali vallunarkaḷ aṉivarum pōṟṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  tiruvalañcuḷiyil uḷḷa tirukkōyiliṉ palakaṇi vēli mika nuṇukkamāṉa ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ amintatu.  aṅku ciṟpikaḷ kalli maram pōl nūl iḻi eṇṇac cetukki ulakam uḷḷaḷavum taṅkaḷ pukaḻ maṅkātu ceytuḷḷaṉar.  koṅku nāṭṭil tāramaṅkalam, tāṭik kompu eṉṉum ūril uḷḷa ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷum aṟputa māṉavikaḷēyākum.  oppaṟṟatu, ulakam kaṇṭu viyakkattakkatu.
intiyāvilē mikap periya atāvatu 218  -aṭi uyaramuḷḷa vimāṉatti ki.pi. 10 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil tañciyil cōḻarkaḷ kallāl ciṟpac ciṟapput tikaḻak kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  ataṉ ucciyil uḷḷa vimāṉattiṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭirukkum piramāntirak kal 80  - ṭaṉ eṭiyuḷḷatu.  nalla ciṟpa vēlippāṭu niṟintatu.  960  -āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ paḻuvāṉa poruḷkaḷit tūkkum poṟikaḷi (Crane -I) kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta paḻaṅkālattil ipperuṅkōyili amittu, viṇmuṭṭum vimāṉatti eḻuppi orē kalliṉālāṉa 80 - ṭaṉ eṭiyuḷḷa peruṅkallit tūkki oru nūl aḷavum vilakātu oru ciṟu pakutiyum uṭiyātu ēṟṟuvittuḷḷatu ciṟpa vallunarkaḷ mūḷikku oru putirāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
intac ciṟpa vēlip pāṭukaḷ ceyaṟkariya nuṇukkam vāyntavi eṉṟu iṉṟu ellāc ciṟpikaḷum ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  iṉṟu kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭivarum ciṟpikaḷ kōyil kaṭṭum celvarkaḷōṭu ceyyum oppantattil, tiru valañcuḻip palakaṇi, tiruvīḻimiḻali vuvāl nattu maṇṭapam, āviṭiyār kōyil koṭuṅki, tāramaṅkalam, tāḻk kompu eṉṉum iru ūril uḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tavirttu ēṉiya ikkōyilic ciṟanta amippilum, ciṟanta ciṟpa vaṭivilum aḻakiyatāy, uṟutiyāy, amittut tarukiṟōm eṉṟu uṟuti moḻi kūṟi uṭaṉpaṭikki eḻuti oppantam ceytu koṭuppatu vaḻakkamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.
itaṉ mūlam paṇṭik kālattu ciṟpācāriyarkaḷiṉ ēṟṟam kuṟippiṭap paṭuvati nām uṇaraṟpālatu.  itaṉik koṇṭu ikkāla ciṟpikaḷ muṟkālac ciṟpācāriyarkaḷiviṭa toḻil tiṟamiyil piṉtaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkilli.  cantarppam aḷittāl nañcentamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻum iṉṟiyac ciṟpikaḷ tamatu muṉṉōrkaḷi viṭa ciṟanta ciṟpa nuṇukkam aṟintavarkaḷāy uḷḷavarkaḷ eṉpatik kāṭṭattayarāy irukkiṉṟārkaḷ paṇṭik kālac ciṟpikaḷi viṭa iṉṟiya ciṟpikaḷ viññāṉa rītiyāṉa arumperum āyutaṅkaḷiyellām peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ.
cumār patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻarkaḷ kaṭṭiṭakkaliyil ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu paṇṭiyat tamiḻnāṭṭup pulavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷati mikippaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭatu.  iṉṟu āṅkilam payiṉṟa varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ cilar maṟutta pōtilum mokañtarō nākarikattiṟku muṉṉar atāvatu 5000 -m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻarkaḷ uyarnta māṭa māḷikikaḷiyum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar eṉpati evarum maṟukkār eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ.  paṇṭiyat tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil ciṟpat toḻilukku iṉṟiyamiyāta uṟuppukaḷāka pattup poruḷkaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.  atāvatu kal, ulōkam, ceṅkal, maram, maṇ, cuti, tantam, vaṇṇam, kaṇṭa carukkari, meḻuku ākiyavikaḷ ākum.  tivākara cūttiram, 
kallu mulōkamum ceṅkalu maramum
maṇṇuñ cutiyum tantamum vaṇṇamum
kaṇṭacark kariyu meḻuku meṉṟivi
pattē ciṟpat toḻiṟkuṟup pāvaṉa		(tivākaram)
kūṟiyiruppatu tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa ciṟpa uṟuppi meyp pikkat takka cāṉṟākum.  mēlum kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta māḷikiyil cuṭumaṇṇāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa uyarnta māṭikaḷiyuṭiya illaṅkaḷilellām veṇcutiyāl tīṭṭappeṟṟa uyir oviyaṅkaḷum, maṇṇiṇālum, kalliṉālum marattiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷ pala amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  eṉṟu maṇimēkaliyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avi varumāṟu,

nāḷ ceytal
kōṉ vāḻum kōyilkaḷum celvarkaḷ vāḻum māṭaṅ kaḷum teyvaṅkaḷ uṟiyum tirukkōyilkaḷum maṇṭapaṅkaḷum ciṟpak kaliyiṟ ciṟanta cīriya pulavarkaḷāl nāḷum kōḷum naṉkaṟintu nēraṟi kayiṟiṭṭu ticikaḷiyum tikkukaḷiyum atticikaḷil niṟkum teyvaṅkaḷiyum maṇṇiyum atil uḷḷa ceṭikaḷiyum nakirarāyntu kaṭṭiṭam amikkum paṇiyit toṭaṅkuvar.  iṭattit tērntu paṭattit tērntu aḷavik kaṇakkiṭṭu kayiṟukaḷiṉ tuṇiyāl naṉkaḷantu iṟivaṉi vēṇṭi tēṅkāy paḻam pū mutaliyavi paṭittu pūci ceytu tamatu paṇiyi ārampippar.
