kalimutalvarkaḷ
ciṟpak kalikku mūla mutalvarkaḷ mayaṉ eṉṟum vicva karmā eṉṟum collappaṭupavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ vaḻi vantavarkaḷē tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kammiyarkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷē ciṟpak kalikaḷiṉ mūla nūṟkaḷi yāttavarkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
mayaṉ
ivarkaḷil mayaṉ acura taccaṉ eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar.  cila nūṟkaḷil ivaṉ teyvakammiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.  kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil uḷḷa uvavaṉattiṉ paḷikkaṟiyil uḷḷa putta pīṭikiyiyum cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattiyum kantiṟpāviyiyum ivaṉē iyaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu maṇimēkali eṉṟa tamiḻk kāppiya nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.  pāratam iṉṉum vaṭamoḻi itikācattil mayaṉ kāṇṭava vaṉattilirunta arccuṉaṉāl kāppāṟṟappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum ivaṉ pāṇṭavarkaḷukku intirap piratam eṉṉum nakariyum pāṇṭavarkaḷ aracu vīṟṟirukkat takka capā maṇṭapattiyum kaṭṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
ivaṉ titiyiṉ makaṉ eṉṟum vicuvakarmaṉ makaṉ eṉṟum vicciravā eṉpavaṉ makaṉ eṉṟum palavāṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  mayaṉuṭiya makkaḷ māyāvi, tuntupi, maṉu mutaliyōr eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  ivaṉ makaḷākiya maṇṭōtariyi irāvaṇaṉ maṇam ceytu koṇṭatāl irāvaṇaṉukku oppaṟṟa aḻakiya araṇmaṉiyiyum kōṭṭikaḷiyum āyutaṅkaḷiyum mayaṉ ākkik koṭuttāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
mayaṉ nākar kulattic cērntavaṉ.  nākatti vaḻipaṭupavaṉ.  irāvaṇaṉ iyakkaṉ civaṉi vaḻipaṭupavaṉ.  intac campantattāl nākar camayamum civaneṟiyum iṇintaṉa.  civaṉ nākatti talimītum kaḻuttilum, kiyilum, iṭuppilum aṇintu koṇṭār.  civaliṅkattiṉ mītu ēḻutali nākam paṭam eṭuttu niṉṟu niḻal tarukiṉṟatu.  palavita iliṅkaṅkaḷil nākaliṅkamum oṉṟu.
nāka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu (atāvatu ilaṅkiyil uḷḷa nākat tīviṉiṉṟu) civaneṟiyit taḻuviya nākarkaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻakattil vāḻum kammiyarkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.  nākarkaḷ civa neṟiyit taḻuvi tamiḻarkaḷōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu viṭṭaṉar.  eṉṟālum tamiḻakattil vāḻum kammiyarkaḷ illattil naṭipeṟum tirumāḷ viḻāvil nākavallimuḷuttam pōṉṟa caṭaṅkukaḷ iruntu varuvatu avarkaḷiṉ paḻiya marapu vaḻakkattik kāṭṭuvatāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  nākar kulamaṅkiyum irāvaṇaṉ maṉiviyumāṉa kammāḷar kula maṅki maṇṭōtari ulakil uḷḷa 10 pativiratikaḷil mutal iṭattip peṟṟavarkaḷ.  avaḷ ciṟanta civa pakti vāyntavaḷ.  tēvāra āciriyarkaḷāl ārkalituḻ teṉ ilaṅki aḻakar maṇṭōtarikkup pēr aruḷa aḷitta pirāṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟap peṟum ēṟṟam peṟṟavarkaḷ.  irāmaṉ maṉivi cītiyi viṭa maṇṭōtari aḻakilum, aṟivilum, paktiyilum, kaṟpilum kuṟṟam kūṟa muṭiyāta mācu maruvaṟṟa pattari māṟṟuc cokkat taṅkamāvāḷ.
vicvakarmaṉ
vicuvakarmā mānti nakaril vāḻum ciṟpikaḷiṉ teyvamākum.  vicuvakarmāviṉ aintumukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu maṉu (kollar) mayaṉ(taccar) tuvaṣṭā(kaṉṉār) cilpā(ciṟpi) vicuvaṉṉā (taṭṭār) ākiya aintu ciṟpa iṉattavarkaḷum tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu vicuva purāṇamum, māntip pāl, vicayatarma nāṭakam pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  intac ciṟpikaḷ muṟkālattil māntiyil āṭcipurinta nākar kulattic cērntavarkaḷāvar.  intac ciṟpa kulattavarkaḷ pērāṟṟalum, miku perumiyum vāyntavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ nāka vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟum nākar neṟiyit taḻuviyavarkaḷ;  naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟavarkaḷ.  itaṉāl ilaṅkiyil āṭcipurinta iyakka aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ paṭṭattaraci
kaḷi inta mānti nakaril vāḻnta nākarkulattil uḷḷa ōviyar kuṭumpattil uḷḷa ciṟpikaḷiṉ peṇmakkaḷiṉiṉṟē tērnteṭuttaṉar.  ilaṅkiyil āṭcipurinta māperum acura aracaṉ cūrapaṉmaṉiṉ (cuppiramaṇiyaṉāl veṉṟu kollappaṭṭa acuraṉiṉ) paṭṭattaraci patuma kōmaḷāvum (teyva kammiyaṉiṉ tirumakaḷum) iyakkar kulattiṉ iṇiyaṟṟa ēntalāy ilaṅkiyi āṇṭa irāvaṇaṉiṉ (irāmaṉāl veṉṟu kollappaṭṭavaṉ)  maṉivi maṇṭōtariyum -mayaṉ eṉṉum nākar kula araca makaḷir
kaḷēyāvar.  nākar aracaṉiṉ (ciṟpakulattavarkaḷiṉ) marumakaṉāy iruntatāl ilaṅkāpurattil irāvaṇaṉukku aḻakiya araṇmaṉiyum, uyarnta uṟutiyāṉa araṇkaḷiyum, puṣpaka vimāṉattiyum amittuk koṭuttaṉar irāvaṇaṉuṭiya ciṟṟappaṉ kupēraṉuṭiya maṉivi cittiralēkā maṟṟoru teyva kammiyaṉiṉ tirumakaḷ.1
ciṟpikaḷiṉ mūtātiyarāka vicvakarmāviṉ vaḻiyil vanta nākarkula ōviyarkuṭumpattiṉiṉṟu ilaṅkiyi āṇṭa iyakkarkula aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ perumikkuriya aracikaḷit tērnteṭuttu maṇam purintu koṇṭaṉar.  iyakka aracarkaḷiṉ, ilaṅkāpurattiluḷḷa mānti nakar vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivanta aracikaḷ uṟinta mānakarāy viḷaṅkiyatu.  eṉavē innakaril nākar kulac ciṟpikaḷ palar kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  itaṉi māntip paḷḷum vicuvavicayatarma nāṭakamum vicuva purāṇamum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vicuvarmā iyakkar aracarkku māntiyil irumpuk kōṭṭikaḷi amittuk koṭuttaṉar.
(a) irupatu kiyuṭiyāṉ ṟaṉakku maṇṭōtariyi
	īntaruṇ mayaṉār paṇṇip paḷḷikaḷāṇṭē
(ā) cūrapaṉmaṉ maṉivi patuma kōmaḷi tanti
	curar puṉivaṟ paṇintu kūvāy kuyilē
(i) cittira rēki yeṉṉum taṉatiṉ makiḻ tēviyē
	teyva kammiyaṉ peṇṇeṉṟē kūvāy kuyilē
						(mānti pak 8-24)
collarumaṭi kaṭōṟum tulaṅkiya māntiyeyti
cittira mēṭi cūḻnta ṭillinaṉ kiraṭutta
uttira tikkiṉmikka vūcik kāntattiṉālē
cuṟṟilumakala nālin tōcaṉi tūramuḷḷa
muttiri mikunta kōṭṭi ciruṭṭittār muttiṉattil
			vicuvapurāṇam pañcakiruttiyakāṇṭam 
 vi. vi 3, 4. mānti g¡.IX
kāntamalik kōṭṭi mēviya
kāvalaṉ pukaḻ kūṟavē			-vicayatarma nāṭakam.
kānta muṟun taṭa matilum kaḻumalarp poḻilumuṟṟa
mānti nakar				      -māntippaḷ
nākarkaḷāṉa ciṟpācāriyarkaḷ varalāṟu purāṇakkatikaḷ āka iruppatiyuṉṉip palar aṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷvatilli.  mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ purāṇak katikaḷi kaṟpaṉik kati (myth)  eṉṟa poruḷil vittu purāṇatti mutaltaraccāṉṟākak koḷva tilli.  āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ pērāṟṟalukkum, peruñ ceyalukkum kali nayattiṟkum kaṭṭiṭak kali mēmpāṭṭiṟkum purāṇac cāṉṟu maṭṭumalla varalāṟṟu cāṉṟumuṇṭu.  intiyāvil uḷḷa cāraṉāta putta palkalik kaḻakattil putta camaya aṟiya vanta hivāṉ cāṅ (Hiouen Ihsang) teṉ intiyāviṟkum, vēṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuñ ceṉṟu putta camaya vaḷarcciyip paṟṟi eḻutiya kuṟippukaḷil nāka nāṭṭu naṅkiyarkaḷiṉ aḻakum, avarkaḷ ayal nāṭṭuk kappalkārarkaḷi ēmāṟṟi aṭitta kaṭaṟkoḷḷikaḷiyum ciṟappāka nākanāṭṭu eḵku kōṭṭiyiyum paṟṟi kūṟiyiruppatu nāka nāṭṭiṉ ciṟpac ciṟappiṟkum nākarikattiṟkum ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu.1
nākarkaḷ eṭuppitta eḵku araṇ ilaṅkiyiṉ kaṭal vaṇika vaḷarccikku iṭiyūṟāka iruppati aṟintu ilaṅki aracaṉ vicayaṉ ati aḻittu viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu cīṉa vaḻippōkkaṉ yuvāṅ cuvāṅ kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. 2 ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭil muṟkālac cōḻa aracaṉ karikāṟ cōḻaṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  karikālaṉ ilaṅkiyi veṉṟu makkaḷiṟ palari ciṟi piṭittuc ceṉṟu kāvirik kariyi uyartta maṇ cumakkac ceytuḷḷāṉ.3 karikāṟ cōḻaṉ īḻa nāṭṭu eḵku araṇi maṭṭumiṉṟi toṅkum pittaḷi araṇiyum, toṅkum cempu araṇiyum aḻittu kirēkkarkaḷ kūṟum tāmpira paraṇi eṉṉum īḻatti veṉṟu cempiyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭatti cūṭṭiyirukkalām eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tāmpiram eṉpatu cempu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, tāmpiraparaṇi nāṭṭikkip paṟṟi tāmpirak kōṭṭiyi aḻittu karikālaṉ cempiyaṉ paṭṭam cūṭiyirukkalām eṉṟum toṅkum cempu, pittaḷi araṇkaḷi avaṉ aḻittuḷḷataṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷateṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.1
vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivanta ciṟpikaḷ poṟkollarkaḷ muṟkālattil māntiyil nāṭu nakar amittu eḵku cempu kōṭṭikaḷ amittu āṭci naṭatti vantuḷḷaṉar.  piritām eṉṉum āciriyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉṟu itaṉi tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ muppattiraṇṭu uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  ivikaḷ aṉittum vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  camīpakālattil ciṟpanūṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟṟu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  cila āṅkilattil kūṭa veḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ciṟpa nūṟkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
1. viccuva tarumam			17. vātu pōtam
2. viccuvēcam				18. vitāram
3. viccuvacāram				19.intiram
4. viruttam					20 vacciram
5. tāvaṭṭam				21 cavumam
6. naḷam					22 viva kācipam
7. mayam					23 makatantiram
8. aṉumāṉ				24 vicālam
9. pāṉu					25 cittiram
10. kaṟpāriyam				26 kāpikālayūpam
11. ciruṭṭam					27 nāmacaṅkiti
12. māṉacāram				28 cāttikam
13. vatuvittiyāpati		29 vivapōtam
14. pārācariyam				30 āticāram
15. ariṭikam					31 vekuccurutam
16. cayittakam					32 māṉapētam
mēṟkaṇṭa ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷākak koḷḷalām.  āṉāl vaṭa intiya ciṟpikaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum nūṟkaḷ cumār pattu ākum.  avikaḷ aṭiyil varuvaṉavākum.
1. vātuvittiyā				7. camara ñāṉa cūtratārā
2. maṉuṣālayacantirikā		8. vivakarmap pirakācam
3. cilparattiṉam			9. matcaya purāṇam
4. mayamatam					10. akkiṉi purāṇam
5. yuktikalpataru				11. cukkira nīti
6. pirukatcamhiti		12. kāciyapa camhitā
ciṟpikaḷ
matcaya purāṇattil muṟkālattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta 18 ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
1. pirku				10 piramaṉ
2. attiri				11 kumāraṉ
3. vaciṣṭaṉ				12 nantīcaṉ
4. viccuvakarmaṉ		13 caṉakaṉ
5. mayaṉ				14 kākā
6. nārataṉ				15 vācutēvaṉ
7. nākaṉa cittu			16 aṉiruttirā
8. vicālakcā			17 cukarā
9. purantaraṉa				18 pirukapati
ivarkaḷiṉ ciṟpa teyvamākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ vicuvakarmāvum, mayaṉum āvar.
ciṟpikaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟku ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil āṟuvaki campiratāyaṅkaḷ kiyāḷappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  avi varumāṟu.
1. viccuvakarma matam 2. vicvamparamatam 3. piṅkaḷa matam 4. utpala matam  5. mayatam 6. parimaḷamatam.
purāṇaṅkaḷilum ākamaṅkaḷilum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷilum ciṟpa nūlāciriyarkaḷ 18 eṉṟum 32 eṉṟum 81 eṉṟum palavakip peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟpikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ vicuvakarmāvum mayaṉum āvar.  vicuvakarmar tēvarkaḷiṉ ciṟpi mayaṉ acurarkaḷiṉ ciṟpi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  maya matam ippoḻutu muṟṟilum orē nūlāka ippoḻutu kiṭikkiṟatu.  āṉāl vicuvakarmāviṉuṭiya nūl pala pakutikaḷāka antantap pakutic ciṟpat toḻilāḷikkeṉṟē taṉiyākat toṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  viccuva karma vātu cātiram eṉṟa nūli tañci caracuvati makāl nūlniliyam cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.  maṟṟac cila pakutikaḷum, eḷitil kiṭikkum. tiruvāṅkūr aracu, vātu vityar 2 maṉuṣyalaya cantirikā 3 -l parattiṉam , 4. mayamatam. 5. yuktikalpataru, 6. pirukat camhiti. 7. camaṇa ñāṉa sacatratāra 8. viccuva karmap pirakācam pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. tañci caracuvati makāl veḷiyīṭṭāḷarum, cakaḷāti kāram, vivakarma vātu cātiram, mayamatam, kāciyapa cilpam, māṉacāram, cilparattiṉam, pirāmmiya cittira kāma cātiram, pōṉṟa nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  iṉṉum avarkaḷ pala nūṟkaḷi veḷiyiṭum muyaṟciyil toṭarntu ceyaṟpaṭṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
pañcāp camakiruta nūl veḷiyīṭṭuk kaḻakam nīṇṭa nāḷ kaḷāka ciṟpa nūṟkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatil atika akkaṟi eṭuttu varukiṟatu.
vaṅkāḷattil kali āyvuttuṟi atika vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu.  aṅku pala aṟiñarkaḷ naṉku payiṉṟu intiyak kali vaḷarccik keṉṟu urūpam (Rupam) kīḻkkāṭṭuk kali (Cercental Art)   eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pattirikikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷip patippittum cīriya toṇṭāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar
ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyar
namatu nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷum, pulavarkaḷum tam peyarkaḷi muṟkālattil viḷamparam ceytu koḷḷa eṇṇiyatilli.  pala uyarnta nūṟkaḷil eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.  cila nūṟkaḷil āciriyar peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa perumpulavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷilē nūṟkaḷi eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  atē pōṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil peruṅkōyilkaḷiyum uyariya upparikkikaḷiyum viṇmūṭṭum vimāṉaṅkaḷiyum, vilimatikka voṇṇāta veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷiyum cilikaḷiyum paṭitta ciṟpikaḷ taṅkaḷ peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭātu vāḻntu maṟintuḷḷaṉar.  iṭik kālattil kōyil kaṭṭuvataṟkup paṇam aḷitta maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, nivantaṅkaḷ aḷitta aracar, aracikaḷ, araciḷaṅkumarikaḷ, celvarkaḷ peyarkaḷ kalveṭṭukaḷilum, ceppēṭukaḷilum tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  āṉāl ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.  camīpattil cila kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ mūlam cila ciṟpikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
ceṉṉiyi aṭuttuḷḷa māmallapurattil uḷḷa mātirik kōyilkaḷi eṭuppitta tapatikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ teṉ intiyak kalveṭṭukaḷ (South Indian Inscription) eṉṟa nūṟkaḷiṉ mūlam  kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  intap peyarkaḷ māmallapurattil uḷḷa makōṉṉata ciṟpac ciṟappu niṟinta cīriya ciṟpak karuvūlaṅkaḷi tamiḻulakiṟku aḷitta ciṟpac celvaṅkaḷiṉ tirup peyarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  appeyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu. 
(1) kēvāta peruntaccaṉ (2) kuṇamallaṉ (3) payyamiḻippāṉ (4) cāta mukkiyaṉ (5) kaliyāṇi (6) tiruvoṟṟiyūr apācaṉ (7) kollaṉ cēmakaṉ.1
ilalitāyar eṉṉum ciṟpāciriyarip paṟṟi taṇṭi eṉṉum āciriyar ki.pi. 8 ām āṇṭil eḻutiya avanti cuntari eṉṉum camakiruta nūlil ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  ilalitāyar māmallapurattu ciṟpa ācāriyaril oruvar.2
ki.pi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil vikkiramātittaṉ 11, eṉṟa aracaṉ kāñciyi veṉṟu kippaṟṟi piṉ aṅkuḷḷa ciṟpi kuṇṭaṉ eṉpavari aḻittuk koṇṭu pōy paṭṭakkal eṉṉum nakaril paramaṉukku oru aḻakiya kōyili eṭuppittāṉ.  ippoḻutu akkōyil iṭapavākaṉar kōyil eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.  inta aracaṉ iraṇṭām vikkiramātittaṉ ciṟpācāriyāric ciṟappittu tiripuvaṉācāriyar eṉṉum paṭṭam cūṭṭiṉāṉ.  ivarukku aṉavarata āccāri eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu.
mutalām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉiṉ āṭciyil purici nakaril tiruppaṭakkāruṭiya mātēvar kōyil aṭippaṭiyiṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.  itaṉik kaṭṭiya ciṟpi cantira cēkaraṉ ravi eṉṉum cōḻēntira cimma āccāri eṉappaṭum.3
cōḻap pēraracil pērum perumiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya māmaṉṉaṉ irācēntiraṉ tiruvoṟṟiyūr mūlak kōyilip paḻutu pārttup putuppittāṉ.  ikkōyilip paḻutupārttup putuppitta ciṟpiyiṉ peyar vīra cōḻa taccaṉ (iravi) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭār.  tilli āṭavallāṉ kōyil vaṭakkuk kōpurattiyum uḷ cuvaril kōpuratti amitta ciṟpiyiṉ peyarum uruvamum eḻuta muṭivu ceytu, viruttakiri, kēcavap perumāḷ makaṉ vicu tiruppiṟik kōṭi ācāri tirumaruṅkaṉ.  avar tampi cāri eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, ampācamuttiram vaṭṭattil uḷḷa piramatēcattil uḷḷa kalāca nāta cuvāmi kōyil vicuvakarmāviṉāl nirmāṇikkappaṭṭatu teṉ intiya kōyiṟ cācaṉattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1

inta atikāram eḻutat 
tuṇiyāy irunta nūṟkaḷ

civañāṉa muṉivar, kumarakuruparar, tirumaṅkiyāḻvār, kulacēkarāḻvār, nammāḻvār, ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyar, vaṇṇaccarapam taṇṭapāṇi aṭikaḷār, eṉṟi ālparṭṭu kiruṭṭiṉār, tirukūṭarācappak kavirāyar .. iṉṉum iṉṉum.. putumippittaṉ, mātaviyā, kā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, viyāpuriyār, pūraṇaliṅkaṉār, cōmacuntara pāratiyār, pāratiyār, umaṟuppulavar.. iṉṉum iṉṉum tamiḻ mīṭpar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar- pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṇivakuppu immāvaṭṭattiṉ ciṟappēyākum. ō!
nuṇkalic celvar cāttaṉkuḷam a.irākavaṉārum piṟantu perumi cērttatu immāvaṭṭamē! nākacuramum, villuppāṭṭum iṅku pukaḻ cērkkum kalikaḷākum.
cāttāṉkuḷam nelliyiliruntu tiruccentūr cellum vaḻiyil uḷḷa ūrākum. vaṭivēṟkavirāyar, kaṉakacapāpatik kavirāyar, kuḻanti vēlcāmi pōṉṟa kaviñarkaḷ piṟantu pukaḻpeṟṟa ūrākum.
nuṇkalic celvar a.irākavaṉār avarkaḷ 22.04.1902 ām āṇṭu tōṉṟiṉār. ivartam peṟṟōr tiruvāḷar aruṇācalak kavirāyar, tiruvāṭṭi āvuṭiyammāḷ āvar. ivaratu pāṭṭaṉār vaṭivēṟkavirāyar ciṟanta muttamiḻppulavar. ivaratu muppāṭṭaṉār vīrapāku ōtuvār mikacciṟanta kaviñar; atōṭu vaḻakkaṟiñarum kūṭa! ivaratu muppāṭṭaṉāriṉ tampi aruṇācalak kavirāyar tiruvaṉantapuram araṇmaṉip pulavarāka oḷi vīciṉār eṉpatu maṭṭumiṉṟi kuṭṭik kavirāyar eṉṟa aṭimoḻiyōṭum vāḻntavar.
nuṇkalic celvariṉ piḷḷit tiruppeyar iratṉa irākava utaya mārttāṇṭaṉ kavirāyar eṉpatākum. piṉṉartāṉ irākavaṉ eṉṟu maṭṭumē aḻikkappaṭṭār.
iḷantip paruvattil piṟanta cāttāṉkuḷattilēyē toṭakkakkalvi payiṉṟār. nelli araciṉar payiṟcip paḷḷiyil āciriyam payiṉṟu āciriyarāṉār. cuppiramaṇiyak kavirāyariṭam tamiḻ muṭutaṟak kaṟṟum, toṇṭimāṉ piramma muttiyaṉiṭam ōviyamum kaṟṟu ciṟanta ōviyarāka viḷaṅkiṉār.

kuṭumpattiṉ viḷivil
cāttāṉkuḷam aruṇācalakkavirāyarukkum āvuṭiyammāḷukkum makkaḷ aṟuvar. ivarē mutal makaṉ. kuṭumpattiṟkum .. koñcu tamiḻukkum ivarē mutalvar. 2. kumāra vaṭivammāḷ 3. vaṭivēl (iḷantiyilē maṟintār) 4. kumāracāmi 5. kāntimati ma.paramacivaṉ .. orē oru peṇmakaḷ. avartām ca. vīralaṭcumi ammiyār. irākavaṉār tāymāmaṉ makaḷiyē tiruvāṭṭi cuntarattammiyāri maṇantu iṉiya illaṟattiṉ naṉkoṭiyāka mallikā, cutantirā,tamiḻaraci, irā.mativāṇaṉ eṉa mūṉṟu peṇmakkaḷiyum, oru āṇmakaṉiyum vaḻaṅkiṉār.
taṉ makkaḷi palar maṟuttapōtum tamiḻa vaḻik kalviyilēyē cērttup paṭikka vittuḷḷār. taṉ taṅkiyār tirumaṇam nīṅkalāka ellāt tirumaṇaṅkaḷiyum cīrtiruttat tirumaṇamākavē naṭattit tamiḻukkup perumi cērttirukkiṟār. tāyiṟ ciṟantoru kōvilumilli eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi colliyum tāyum mītu mikunta pācam koṇṭiruntār. cīvāṉantam, cāntam cuntaravaṭivēlu- pōṉṟa ivarōṭu naṭpuk koṇṭu tamiḻil koṇṭuḷḷa aiyappāṭukaḷi aṟintu koḷ
vārkaḷām. kuṟaḷ oppuvittāl - kācu koṭuttu ūkkappaṭuttip pārāṭṭuvatu ivaratu iṉiya vāḻviyalākum. atu iḷañciṟār
kaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭumām!
ayalakak kaḷippil
ilaṅki, malēciyā, ciṅkappūr pōṉṟa ayalakaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa nuṇkalic celvar tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷāṉa eṅkum cellum kācu.. kā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, tīntamiḻppulavar tiru.vi.kaliyāṇa
cuntaraṉār, tanti periyār, tārūl iculam āciriyar pā.tāvūtcā, ilaṅki mutalamiccar paṇṭāranāyaka, kaṇṭi mēyar cārc-ṭi-celvā, amiccar peri.cuntaram , mēyar caravaṇamuttu, tamiḻaka camaṉmic cintaṉiyāḷar pa.cīvāṉantam pōṉṟōrōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu avarkaḷāl paṉmuṟi pārāṭṭap peṟṟār.
1935 ām āṇṭu periyāriṭamiruntu vilaki tōḻar pa.cīvāṉantattōṭu cērntu cuyamariyāti camatarmak kaṭci eṉṟoru kaṭciyi niṟuvi aṟivu eṉṉum tiṅkaḷēṭṭiṟkam āciriyarāṉār. 1932il ilaṇṭaṉ pakuttaṟivu caṅkam, viṭutalic cintaṉiyāḷar maṉṟam, amerikkāviluḷḷa uṇmi nāṭuvōr kaḻakam,pōṉṟa amippuk
kaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu uṟuppiṉarāki palvēṟu nūlkaḷi moḻiyākkam ceytu tantār. ilaṅkiyil maṉitaruḷ māṇikkam nēru perumakaṉukku āṅkilattilum, tamiḻilum niṉivu malar veḷiyiṭap peritum utaviṉār. ciṅkappūriliruntu veḷivarum tamiḻ muracu eṉṟa itaḻil toṭarkaṭṭuri eḻuti vara ataṉ āciriyā kō.cāraṅkapāṇi vēṇṭiṉār. tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭil tāmari eṉṟa ivaratu nūl iḷaṅkali vakuppukaḷukkup pāṭanūlāka maturip palkalikkaḻakam ēṟṟup pukaḻ koṇṭatu.
āyviṉ kūrmiyil
avaratu ūrukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa ātittanallūrukkuc ceṉṟum, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷip pārviyiṭṭum, ceṉṉi aruṅkāṭciyakam ceṉṟum āyvu ceytutāṉ ātittanallūrum poruniveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa nūli eḻutiyuḷḷār.
cintu veḷi nākarikattiṟku oppiṭṭup pārttu ērāḷamāṉa karuvikaḷ, poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ paṭaṅkaḷi, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, viḷakkukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ paṭaṅkaḷit tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu .. ivaratu paṉimali muyaṟcip pāṅkiṉi- namakku uṇarttukiṟatu.
yārumē ceyyāta putumiyāka nuṇkalic celvar avarkaḷ taṉ maṉiviyār tiruvāṭṭi cuntarattammiyārik koṇṭu kalinūṟ patippakam niṟuvi pala nūlkaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻp paṇi ceytār eṉpatu ulaka varalāṟṟil putumiyākum.
tammiṭamirunta ērāḷamāṉa kācukaḷ, nūlkaḷ, ariya poruḷkaḷ - ākiyavaṟṟit tirucciyiluḷḷa īpar kaṇkāṇiyar kalvi niṟuvaṉat
tiṟku vaḻaṅkiṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. avi ariya kalip poruḷkaḷākat tulaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ilaṅkiyil oru ciṉṉa nūl antak kālattil nūṟu urūpā koṭuttu vāṅkiyuḷḷār eṉil avaratu aṟivut tākam namakkup pulappaṭum.
pārāṭṭut tēṉmaḻiyil
1966 ām āṇṭu teṉṉintiya tamiḻc caṅkam, caṭṭap pēravit talivar māṇpati cellappāṇṭiyaṉār talimiyil tamiḻttiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār ivarukkup poṉṉāṭi pōrtti nuṇkalic celvar eṉṟa paṭṭatti vaḻaṅkip pārāṭṭiyatu.
1937ām āṇṭu ilaṅkiyil naṭipeṟṟa mukammatu napi ulaka camātāṉattiṟkuc ceyta toṇṭu eṉṟa talippil naṭipeṟṟa kaṭṭurip pōṭṭiyil ivar taṅkap patakkam peṟṟār. 
1966-ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭu malēciyāvil naṭipeṟṟa pōtu tamiḻnāṭṭuk kācukaḷ eṉṟa kaṭṭuri mikac ciṟanta kaṭṭuriyākap pōṟṟappaṭṭatu.
ceṉṉiyil naṭipeṟṟa ulakat tamiḻmānāṭṭil paḻaṅkāla marakkalaṅkaḷ 46 vaṭivaṅkaḷi neymavaṇṇattil (Oil pint) variyap peṟṟuk kāṭcikkākki pēraṟiñar aṇṇāvē peritum pōṟṟiyum, pārāṭṭiyum makiḻntār.

paḻaṅkālam nūl veḷiyiṭum kuḻuvil ivarum oru uṟuppiṉar eṉpatu niṉinta pōṟṟattakkatu.

1.	kāñcippurāṇam
1.	  This shrine, which is dedicated to the worship of Shiva (“warahareswara literally means the god who wards off fever”), lies between the Ekambardesvara and pandavaperumal temple;  it is easily distinguished  from the others by circular garbhagraha (akanāḻiki) originally built of cut granite without cement, it has had to be repired by the Archacological survey of India. 
	   Some of the Characteristie decorative features of later chola art, such as the kumbha panjaram (a kind of plaster with an ornate vase below) and the tarangabodigi (a curved corbel) are found in this temple.  Both the Shrine and the mahamandpa may be assigned to the reign of kulottunga 111 (1178 - 1226) - KANCHI An Introduction to its Architecture Miss Dr. C. Meenakshi M.A.Ph.D. (Published by The publication Division govt of India New Delhi. February 1954.

1.  		  The village Pennadam appears in inscription under the names pennagadam and Mudikonda Chadurvedi mangalam and the diety is called Thiruthoongani Madamudayar apparently from the formation of the Garpagraha in the Gajaprista style which resembles the hind portion is popularly deveved from the tradition that the God here was worshipped by a grandarva lady (pen) a cow (AA) & an alephant (kadam).  (peṇ+ā+kaṭam) South Indian Epigraphy Vol II.

1.	Reporting his recovery from the drift sand in the vicinity of what he called a trisula head of Siva, Longhurst Concluded that this was the object of worship in this pratha in the place of Linga.  He also referred to the similar head (with a trident) decorating the Southern end of the roof of Ganesa ratha.  He was also of opinion that the worship of the trident head was probably a contemporary culb.  A guide to Mamallapuram - Miss. P. T. Narayanan B.A.B.T (Madras) -1957 P 48.)
1.	    In spite of this Mr. Longhurst classifies both caves among those carved in reign of Narasimhavarman I. Perhaps he is led to this conculssion by the short inscription found in the Dharmaraja’s Mandapa, viz. Atyantakama Pallavesvaragrha that is “the temple of the Pallava Lord Atyantakama.  Thus on the Dhavarajas Ratha which undoubtedly belongs to be regin of Narasimha Varman Mamallan there are two names Narasimha and Atyantakama the later in floried stye totally different from the style of the first studies in Pallava History - Rev. H. Heras. P.77.

Dravida buildings exist today not only in Dravida - desha (country itself but also in the Kanarese districts of Decan where in fact, the temples are several centuries older than the earliest structural temples of the Pallavas in Mamallapuram and Conjeevaram” - The Arts and Crafts of Travancore - (Chap 1. 1.	The tamil races were perhaps the greatest Temple builders in the world..... the great pagoda of  Tanjore..... by far the grandest temple in India”.... “Architecture” - Encyclopedia of Britanica 9th edition 11. P.396.
1.	   “The great Temple of Tanjore”.  This style arose under the chola or Tanjore kings in 11th century A.D when nearly all the great temples to Siva in South India were built and it continued in use in the 12th and 18th 
	centuries during which the great temples to Vishnu were erected Rep to the begining of the 16th century these temples remined almost unchanged, but at that time all South India became subject to the kings of Vijayanagar and one of these named Krishna Raya (1509-30) re built or added to most of the great temples of the South.  The chief feature of the architecture of this later period is the construction of the enormous gopurams which are so conpienos
	at Kancheepuram Chidambaram and Srirangam.  All these were built by Krishna - raya : they do not form part of the original style, but were intended as fortifications to protect the shrines from foreign unvaders and certin plunder and desecration, as the Hindu first discovered on the Muhammadan invasion of 1310 A.D also vide, “Three Main styles of Temple Architecture” - Burnell CA Bulletin of the Madras Museum.

1.	Ancient Jaffna - Mudaliar C. Rasanayagam C.C.S (Cylon) 1926 Madras.
1.	   “The Records of the Buddhist religion say:- In the middle of  a great iron city of this Ratnadvipa (Pao - Chu) was the dwelling of the Rakohasi women (Lo - l’sa).  On the towers of this city they erected two high flagstaffs with  lucky or unlucky signals which the exhibited (to allow manners) according to circumstances when merchants came to the island (Ratnadvipa) Then they changed themselves into beautiful women holding flowers and scents, and with the sound of Music they went forth to meet theries and caressingly invited them to enter the iron city then having shared with them all sorts pleasures they shut them up in an iron prison and devoured them at their lesisure”
Hiouen M.L.R Vol : i p : 148.
